#### The contents-list traps

- **D25. Two contents-list names are active traps for this archive and must not be mapped.**
  - **"باب ۷۰ حضرت حاجی وارث علی شاہ ۳۷۸" (p. 16) must not be mapped to mausoleum-of-waris-shah.** The
    archive row is **Waris Shah** the poet at Jandiala Sher Khan, Sheikhupura, d. 1798, Chishti. The
    contents names **حاجی وارث علی شاہ**, Haji Waris Ali Shah — a different name, with the honorific
    **حاجی** and the element **علی**. **The book gives no town and no date. A name-similarity match here
    would put the wrong person in a Pakistani row.**
  - **"باب ۴۰ حضرت شاہ کمال کیتھلی ۲۰۴" (p. 16) must not be mapped to shrine-of-baba-shah-kamal.** The
    nisba is **کیتھلی**, of Kaithal; the archive row is **Baba Shah Kamal** at Muslim Town / Samanabad,
    **Lahore**, Qadiri. **The book gives no Lahore, no date and no order. Leave unmapped.** (Chunk 001
    records that the sibling tazkirah's chunk 012 flagged the identical trap for the identical name.)
- **D26. Contents-list names that resemble archive rows and are locators only — none mappable from the
  contents, each must wait for the chunk that carries the actual entry** (pp. 15–16):
  "باب ۷ حضرت شیخ بہاؤالدین زکریا ۴۴" (→ **shrine-of-bahauddin-zakariya**; the entry itself, in this
  range, does map — see the row above); "باب ۸ حضرت بابا فرید الدین مسعود گنج شکرؒ ۵۲" (→
  **shrine-of-fariduddin-ganjshakar**; see D1); "باب ۱۷ حضرت شیخ رکن الدین ابو الفتح ۱۰۹" (→
  **shrine-of-shah-rukn-e-alam**; see D22); "باب ۵۰ حضرت مادھو لال حسین ۲۴۵" (→ **madho-lal-hussain**);
  "باب ۵۱ حضرت شاہ ابو المعالی ۲۵۱" (→ **darbar-abul-muali-qadri**); "باب ۵۳ حضرت میاں میر ۲۶۵" (→
  **shrine-of-mian-mir**); "باب ۶۰ حضرت سلطان باہو ۳۰۹" (→ **garh-maharaja-shorkot**) — **all four Lahore
  or Jhang rows, all four plausible, and the contents says nothing but the name**; "باب ۶۷ حضرت خواجہ
  محمد سلیمان ۳۵۹" (→ **darbar-hazrat-khawaja-shah-muhammad-sulaiman-taunsvi-r-a**; **no Taunsa and no
  date, and the name alone fits many**); "باب ۵۴ حضرت شاہ بلاول ۲۷۴" (bears on
  **shah-noorani-shrine-syed-bilawal-shah-noorani** only through the element **بلاول**; **the book's name
  has no "Noorani" and no Balochistan — do not map**).
- **D27. "باب ۲۲ حضرت سید جلال الدین بخاری ۱۲۹" (p. 15) could be either of two archive rows at Uch
  Sharif** — **shrine-of-jalaluddin-surkh-posh-bukhari** (d. 1291) or
  **shrine-of-makhdoom-jahaniyan-jahangasht** (Sayyid Jalaluddin, d. 1384), whose archive names share both
  "Sayyid Jalaluddin" and "Bukhari". **Do not pick one.**
- **D28. Contents-list names that differ from the names printed at the chapter openings**, all inside this
  range: contents "حضرت شیخ احمد نہرانی[OCR?]" (p. 15) against the heading "حضرت شیخ احمد نہروانی" (p. 46,
  folio 42); contents "حضرت شیخ شرف الدین احمد یحیٰ ہندی[OCR?]" (p. 15) against the heading and the
  saint's own self-naming "احمد یحیٰی منیری" (p. 97, folio 93); contents "حضرت خواجہ محمود موینہ دوز"
  (p. 15) against the heading "حضرت خواجہ محمود موئینہ دوز" (p. 43, folio 39); contents "حضرت شیخ نظام
  الدین ابو الموید" (p. 15) against the heading "حضرت شیخ نظام الدین ابو الموئد" (p. 72, folio 68).
  **Cite the form with its page.**

#### Other names in the range that collide with archive rows and must not be mapped

- **D29. The "شمس" of باب 14 must not be mapped to shrine-of-shah-shams-ud-din-sabzwari** (Shah Shams-ud-Din
  Sabzwari at **Multan**, d. 1276) nor to **shamsabad** (Shams Ali Qalandar, Okara, d. 1966): this saint's
  mazar is at **Panipat** and he died **716 AH** (p. 94, folio 90). **Name only, nothing else.**
- **D30. حضرت شیخ شرف الدین بو علی قلندر must not be mapped to lal-shahbaz-qalandar** (Sehwan Sharif,
  d. 1274, Qalandari) on the strength of the shared title **"قلندر"**, nor to **shamsabad**. **The archive
  has no row at Panipat** (pp. 104–108, folios 100–104).
- **D31. حضرت خواجہ قطب الدین بختیار کاکی must not be mapped to tomb-of-qutbuddin-aibak.** That row is
  **Sultan Qutb ud-Din Aibak** at Anarkali, **Lahore**, d. 1210, a ruler, not this shaikh; **the shared
  first two elements of the name make this a likely future mis-match** (pp. 35–37, 43, 46, 57–61, 73, 105;
  folios 31–33, 39, 42, 53–57, 69, 101).
- **D32. حضرت شیخ رکن الدین فردوسی must not be mapped to shrine-of-shah-rukn-e-alam** (Multan, d. 1335,
  Suhrawardi): **the nisba here is فردوسی** and the range gives no place or date (p. 97, folio 93).
- **D33. حضرت شیخ جمال الدین ہانسوی must not be mapped to shrine-of-hafiz-muhammad-jamal-multani**
  (Multan, d. 1811, Chishti): the book's figure died **659ھ** with his mazar at **ہانسی** (p. 85,
  folio 81; and p. 104, folio 100).
- **D34. حضرت شیخ صدر الدین عارف has no archive row and is a candidate for one.** The book states his
  death as "23 ذی الحجہ 684ھ" and his "مزار ملتان میں ہے" (p. 75, folio 71) — **a dated Pakistani shrine,
  with named khalifas, a named sajjada nashin and a quantified patrimony, none of which the 169 rows
  carry.** Chunk 004 flags him explicitly as a candidate row.

#### Standing cautions for the whole range

- **Only three archive rows are engaged in 109 pages, and one of those three is engaged only through a
  disputed mapping.** The author is an **Indian** custodian writing from Ajmer about "پاک و ہند" (pp. 1,
  7, 18); the contents list spreads his seventy saints across both countries (pp. 15–16); **every later
  chunk must check the town before mapping**, because the name overlaps with Pakistani rows are dense and
  at least two of them point at the wrong person (D25).
- **The book's coverage is the residue of two earlier volumes** (p. 18). **An absence in this book is not
  evidence of anything.**
- **The author reports variants and does not correct them** (see Cross-cutting material). **Any entry
  drawing on this book must carry the variant, not the primary reading alone** — this applies to Hujwiri's
  465ھ/456ھ (p. 23, folio 19), Farid's 575ھ/569ھ and 670ھ/664ھ (pp. 56, 62; folios 52, 58), Bahauddin
  Zakariya's two maternal grandfathers (p. 49, folio 45), and Bu Ali Qalandar's five bai'ats (p. 105,
  folio 101).
- **No Common-Era date appears anywhere in pp. 1–109.** Every date in the range is Hijri, except the
  colophon's "اکتوبر 1999ء" and the preface's "۴، اپریل ۱۹۶۵ء [OCR?]" (pp. 8, 18). **No era conversion
  may be attributed to this book.**

### pp. 110–210 (chunks 006–010)

#### Chapter numbering and the chunk seams

- **باب 27 never appears and "باب 28" is printed twice.** The sequence as transcribed runs باب 26
  (p. 167, folio 163) → **باب 28** (p. 169, folio 165, Ahmad Abdul Haq Rudaulvi) → **باب 28** again
  (p. 175, folio 171, Shah Badi al-Din Madar) → باب 29 (p. 182, folio 178). **Neither number carries an
  `[OCR?]` flag**, so both are recorded as printed; one of the two is presumably 27 in the book. **A
  human must resolve which chapter number belongs to which saint against the PDF**, because the merged
  file will cite chapters.
- **باب 21's subject is established across the seam between chunks 006 and 007, and both notes name
  him.** chunk 006 gives the heading "باب 21 حضرت شیخ جلال الدین محمد کبیر الاولیاء" at p. 127
  (folio 123) and records the entry breaking off at p. 128 (folio 124) mid-sentence, "انہوں نے آپ کو
  دیکھتے ہی حاضرین سے فرمایا", with the speech not following; chunk 007 records the close of an entry
  at pp. 129–132 (folios 125–128) whose heading falls outside it and whose subject is named in running
  text as "حضرت شیخ جلال الدین کبیر الاولیاء" (p. 130, folio 126) and implied by "ہمارے نام پر اس کا نام
  جلال آباد رکھو" (p. 131, folio 127). **The two notes agree on the name, so the entry may be treated as
  continuous pp. 127–132** — but note that its footnote markers 1–6 are not in pp. 129–132 (the first
  present is 7 at p. 129, folio 125) while the حواشی block at p. 132 (folio 128) lists twelve, all from
  "سیر الاقطاب (فارسی)" pp. 197–211.
- **The unnamed entry at pp. 148–150 is probably the tail of باب 23, but no note states it and a human
  must confirm.** chunk 007 records باب 23 as حضرت میر سید اشرف جہانگیر سمنانی beginning at p. 145
  (folio 141) and **continuing past the end of that chunk** at p. 147 (folio 143), with its حواشی block
  falling outside those pages; chunk 008 records an entry with **no name, no nisba and no chapter
  heading** closing at pp. 148–150 (folios 144–146) and ending in its حواشی block, with باب 24's heading
  appearing only at p. 151 (folio 147). The internal evidence chunk 008 lists is consistent with Ashraf
  Jahangir: the takhallus **"اشرف"** (p. 148, folio 144), **seven of nine footnotes to لطائف اشرفی** in
  three forms of its title (p. 150, folio 146), and the qalandar's question about his **"جہانگیری"**
  (p. 150, folio 146) — and the two places named on those pages, **محمد پور** and **ظفر آباد**, are the
  same two Ashraf Jahangir passes through at pp. 146–147 (folios 142–143), where a **مولوی سید خاں** of
  محمد پور also appears (p. 146, folio 142 and p. 149, folio 145). **This is an inference from two chunk
  notes read together, not a statement either of them makes.** Until it is confirmed against the PDF,
  **the آسیب-cure practice and the four karamat of pp. 148–150 remain unattached to any person or
  place** — which matters, because the آسیب line is the only present-tense shrine practice in the whole
  range.
- **The unnamed entry tail at p. 166 is probably the tail of باب 25, on the same kind of evidence, and
  needs the same confirmation.** chunk 008 records باب 25 (Gesu Daraz) filling pp. 153–165 (folios
  149–161) and **breaking off mid-prescription** inside "ذکر اجابت دعوت" at p. 165 (folio 161), with
  **fifteen footnote markers and no حواشی block**; chunk 009 records p. 166 (folio 162) carrying a
  healing formula of the same azkar-with-ضرب type under "ذکر دفع امراض **استقام[OCR?]**", a karamat
  about Timur and Delhi, **footnote marker 16 in the text** and a **sixteen-item حواشی block**, with the
  heading باب 26 appearing only at p. 167 (folio 163). **Neither note makes the identification.** Until
  it is confirmed, **the healing formula and the Timur warning of p. 166 must not be attributed to any
  saint** (p. 166, folio 162). Note also that chunk 009's own doubt records that the whole footnote
  apparatus of that entry sits in chunk 009 while its text does not.
- **باب 16's subject is unresolved within this range.** The chapter is already running at p. 110
  (folio 106) and the only name the pages give him is the parenthetical "آپ (حضرت قلندر صاحب)" (p. 111,
  folio 107); the heading, his full name, his dates and his shrine all fall **before** p. 110. **Do not
  name him from the surrounding figures** — Shams al-Din Turk Panipati appears in his karamat as a
  separate person (p. 111, folio 107), and a "بو علی قلندر" appears only much later, in باب 21
  (pp. 127–128, folios 123–124). A human must read chunk 005 for the heading.
- **The range closes mid-entry.** باب 40 breaks off at "آپ کے مشہور خلفاء حسب ذیل ہیں۔" (p. 210,
  folio 206): **the khalifa list itself is absent**, and so are the حواشی for باب 40, whose markers 1
  and 2 both appear in the text. Chapters 23 and 25 also run past their chunk's edge (pp. 147, 165;
  folios 143, 161), and باب 22's حواشی block at p. 142 (folio 138) is the only complete apparatus for a
  mapped entry in the range.

#### Placement doubts — names that could match two archive rows

- **"حضرت سید جلال الدین بخاری" is the range's principal placement risk, and the book contradicts itself
  about whether he is one person or two.** باب 32 makes him a single figure: "مخدوم جہانیاں جہاں گشت
  حضرت سید جلال الدین بخاری", whose **پوتا** Qutb Alam is (p. 190, folio 186); باب 33 confirms it,
  "حضرت سید جلال الدین بخاری المعروف بہ مخدوم جہانیاں جہاں گشت" (p. 191, folio 187); but باب 35 makes
  them **two generations**, "مخدوم جہانیاں جہاں گشت کے **بیٹے** حضرت سید جلال الدین بخاری", of whom Shah
  Alam is the پر پوتا (p. 195, folio 191). The name matches **shrine-of-makhdoom-jahaniyan-jahangasht**
  element for element, and the bare name also matches
  **shrine-of-jalaluddin-surkh-posh-bukhari-jalaluddin-bukhari**. chunk 010 left him unmapped on two
  grounds: those pages give him **no mazar, no town, no death year and no age** (the only affiliation
  statement is Shah Ajmal's bai'at "چشتیہ جمانیہ[OCR?] و قادریہ و سہروردیہ", p. 191, folio 187, where
  سہروردیہ agrees with both rows and the other two appear in neither), and the book's own genealogy
  contradicts itself. **Both readings are kept above. A human should settle which Uch row, if either, is
  meant, and should also settle باب 35's generation count** — on باب 32's reckoning Shah Alam is the
  great-grandson of Jahaniyan himself; on باب 35's wording he is one generation further down.
- **مخدوم جہانیاں جہاں گشت at p. 116 (folio 112) was deliberately left unmapped by chunk 006**, where he
  is listed among Rukn al-Din's khalifas as "حضرت سید جلال الدین بخاری ملقب بہ مخدوم جہانیاں جہاں گشت"
  with **no location, no death year and no shrine**. chunk 007 then maps the same title as the subject
  of باب 22 on birth at Uch and Suhrawardi bai'at (p. 133, folio 129; p. 135, folio 131). **These two
  decisions are inconsistent with each other as they stand and are recorded side by side above**; the
  entry accepts chunk 007's mapping, and this note preserves chunk 006's refusal.
- **مخدوم جہانیاں at p. 183 (folio 179) was likewise left unmapped by chunk 009**, where he is named
  **only** as the pir and khalifa-maker of Shaikh Qawam al-Din, with no town, no mazar, no death year
  and no silsila; chunk 009 notes that the frame of those pages is Indian throughout, the disciple's
  disciple's mazar being near **لکھوء[OCR?]**. Recorded, unmerged.
- **"شیخ جلال" cannot be resolved and must not be mapped.** Raju Qattal promises Sarang "شیخ جلال کے
  تبرک" and later delivers "حضرت شیخ جلال کا تبرک" (p. 182, folio 178), with **no nisba, no town, no
  date**. Two candidates are live: **شیخ جلال الدین کبیر الاولیاء of پانی پت**, named twelve pages
  earlier in the same chunk (p. 171, folio 167), and
  **shrine-of-jalaluddin-surkh-posh-bukhari-jalaluddin-bukhari** of Uch Sharif. Leave the tabarruk
  attributed to "شیخ جلال" as printed.
- **"خواجہ غریب نواز" here must not be mapped to `khawaja-feroz-ud-din-gharib-nawaz`.** This range's
  Khwaja Gharib Nawaz is the saint whose **دربار and مزار مبارک are at اجمیر** (p. 205, folio 201) and
  on whose mazar the nazrana money was spent (p. 206, folio 202). The archive row of that name is
  "Darbar Hazrat Khawaja Feroz-ud-Din Gharib Nawaz Chishti Nizami", Miani Sahib Graveyard, Lahore, died
  November 27, 1981. **The title alone is the whole of the resemblance**, and the archive has no Ajmer
  row.
- **"حضرت شاہ کمال کتھیلی[OCR?]" must not be mapped to `shrine-of-baba-shah-kamal`.** The chunk's own
  statements exclude it: he arrives in Hindustan at **ٹھٹھ[OCR?]**, passes through **ملتان** and
  **لدھیانہ** to **پائل (سرہند کے قریب)** and thence to **کتھیل[OCR?]**, which he makes his centre, and
  "آپ کا مزار مبارک کتھیل[OCR?] میں" is where the book puts his shrine (pp. 209–210, folios 205–206).
  The archive row is at "Muslim Town / Samanabad (near Ichhra), Lahore", with no death year and silsila
  Qadiri; this saint is born "7 شوال 835ھ", dies "19 جمادی الثانی 921ھ", and his pir carries the nisba
  "قادری" (pp. 208–210, folios 204–206). **Name and Qadiri nisba agree; town, death year and everything
  else do not.**
- **"مولانا شرف الدین کیتھلی[OCR?]", a teacher of Gesu Daraz at Delhi** (p. 155, folio 151), shares the
  کیتھلی nisba with the same known trap and has **nothing** to do with `shrine-of-baba-shah-kamal`.
- **"حضرت شیخ فتح اللہ لواودھی"** (p. 151, folio 147) shares only the element "فتح" with
  `wadpagga-sharif`'s "Ghazi Syed Shah Fateh Muhammad Bukhari" of Peshawar — a different name, a
  different place, and not a match.
- **سیوستان must not be attached to the archive's Sehwan rows on the strength of p. 136 (folio 132)**,
  where it appears only as the jurisdiction of a state office, not as a shrine, a residence or a burial
  place.
- **Baha al-Din Zakariya is mapped on a nisba and a single placing sentence.**
  **shrine-of-bahauddin-zakariya** takes no date, no shrine and no urs from this range; the agreeing data
  are the repeated "ملتانی" (pp. 113–114, folios 109–110), the family Suhrawardi khirqa (p. 114,
  folio 110) and the sentence putting his murid's bai'at at Multan (p. 133, folio 129). **If a stricter
  standard is wanted, this map should be demoted to a mention** — chunk 006 says so in those words.
- **shrine-of-shah-rukn-e-alam's mapping was contested and then made on one sentence.** chunk 004 left
  the figure unmapped; chunk 006 mapped him on grandfather + Multan mazar + Suhrawardi khirqa (pp. 113,
  114, 116; folios 109, 110, 112) and chunk 007 added the independent locational statement at p. 134
  (folio 130). **The chunk never calls him Rukn-e-Alam**: the book gives name **رکن الدین**, kunya
  **ابو الفتح**, laqab **"فضل اللہ"** (p. 113, folio 109) and no "رکن عالم" anywhere in pp. 110–210.
- **The row's death years and the book's are not the same kind of statement, in two cases.**
  shrine-of-shah-rukn-e-alam carries `figure_died` = **1335** against the book's **"16' رجب 735ھ"**
  (p. 116, folio 112); shrine-of-makhdoom-jahaniyan-jahangasht carries **1384** against the book's
  **"10' ذی الحجہ 788ھ"** (p. 137, folio 133). **No conversion has been made in either case and none
  should be inferred**; a human with the calendar tables should settle whether these are the same years.
- **"حاجی شیخ صدر الدین عارف" appears in Rukn al-Din's khalifa list** (p. 116, folio 112) — the same
  name as his father and pir. The book does not remark on it, and this range gives no way to tell
  whether this is a second person of the same name or a duplication.
- **"شیخ قوام الدین" appears in four places and may or may not be one man.** A khalifa of Rukn al-Din
  (p. 135, folio 131); Shaikh Sarang's pir, himself the murid and khalifa of Makhdoom Jahaniyan
  (pp. 182–183, folios 178–179); Shah Ajmal's khirqa-giver, "حضرت قاضی شیخ قوم الدین دہلوی" respelled
  "حضرت قاضی شیخ قوام الدین دہلوی" in the next sentence and made a murid and khalifa of Nasir al-Din
  Chiragh Dehlvi (p. 191, folio 187); and Shah Mina's benefactor, with **no nisba and no place**
  (pp. 192–193, folios 188–189). **The book asserts no connection, and neither spelling is
  OCR-flagged.**
- **Two distinct figures carry the laqab "قطب عالم" and the book never distinguishes them.** باب 32's
  is "سید برہان الدین", died 857ھ, mazar at بتوہ[OCR?] (p. 190, folio 186); باب 37's pir is "حضرت شیخ
  نور الدین قطب عالم" (p. 198, folio 194), and his own stated death year, 822ھ, precedes the other's.
- **Two "حسام الدین" and two "شیخ کبیر" in the same chunks, unconnected by the book.** The سہروردیہ elder
  Husam al-Din staying with Shaikh Sarang (p. 183, folio 179) against مولانا حسام الدین سلامتی named in
  Shah Madar's bequest (p. 177, folio 173); and شیخ کبیر who takes bai'at from Ashraf Jahangir at
  ظفر آباد (p. 147, folio 143) against شیخ کبیر the pir of Khwaja Husain Nagauri (pp. 205–206, folios
  201–202).
- **Whether the two Firoz Shahs are one man is not stated.** "فیروز شاہ" is the king Ahmad Khan
  Khankhanan defeated (p. 168, folio 164); "سلطان فیروز شاہ" is Shaikh Sarang's sovereign and the father
  of the man who married Sarang's sister (p. 182, folio 178); "سلطان فیروز شاہ"/"سلطان فیروز تغلق" is
  the Panipat saint's and Makhdum Jahaniyan's devotee (pp. 129, 137; folios 125, 133); and "سلطان فیروز
  شاہ بہمنی" is a separate ruler in the Deccan (p. 155, folio 151). **The book does not connect or
  distinguish them.**
- **Ahmad Abdul Haq's silsila must not be read backwards up the chain.** The book names "سلسلہ احمدیہ
  چشتیہ" for the order descending through his son (p. 173, folio 169) but attaches **no order-name** to
  Jalal al-Din Kabir al-Auliya or to Shams al-Din Turk. Likewise **no silsila is named for Shah
  Nematullah Wali, Shah Madar or Shaikh Sarang**, nor for Qazi Shihab al-Din Daulatabadi, Shaikh Ahmad
  Khattu, Qutb Alam, Shah Mina, Shaikh Sa'd al-Din Khairabadi, Husam al-Din Manikpuri, Khwaja Husain
  Nagauri or Shah Kamal.
- **The id string for the Uch Surkh-Posh row is written two ways in the notes** —
  `shrine-of-jalaluddin-surkh-posh-bukhari-jalaluddin-bukhari` (chunk 007) and
  `shrine-of-jalaluddin-surkh-posh-bukhari` (chunk 006). A human should check which the index carries
  before the merged file is written.

#### Facts in this range that could not be placed

Distinct facts that exist in the notes but cannot be attached to an archive row, a person or a place.
**Count: 18.**

1. **The whole content of باب 16** — eight اقوال on ishq, husn and self-knowledge, one karamat, sixteen
   footnotes — belongs to a saint the pages never name (pp. 110–112, folios 106–108).
2. **"باگھوٹے [OCR?]"**, the place to which the Qalandar Sahib moved, glossed only as "شہر سے باہر ایک
   جگہ کا نام" — **which city is not stated** (p. 111, folio 107).
3. **Husam al-Din Sokhta's annual urs**, the only urs at a tomb in the range, hangs on the mazar at
   **"سانبھر [OCR?]"**, for which **no district, region or country is given** (p. 124, folio 120). Do not
   place this shrine and do not attach the urs to any archive row.
4. **The town of the three mazars of the Eid-night vigil** — Baha al-Din Zakariya's, Sadr al-Din Arif's
   and Rukn al-Din Abu al-Fath's — is **not printed**, and an `[OCR?]` flag sits on the word "اور"
   joining the last two names, so it is not certain whether a third name or a title is lost there
   (p. 133, folio 129). Multan for these mazars comes only from the grandfather's migration sentence
   earlier on the same page, not from this one.
5. **Makhdum Jahaniyan's own grave.** The وفات rubric gives a date and nothing else; **the range never
   locates his mazar** (p. 137, folio 133), and gives him no urs, no sajjada nashin and no endowment.
6. **Jamal al-Din Khandan's mazar**, the site of the three-step cure prescribed for Sumra — **town not
   stated** (p. 141, folio 137), and no archive row carries his name.
7. **The آسیب-cure practice at an unnamed mazar** — "آج بھی آپ کا مزار آسیب کو دور کرنے کے لئے مشہور
   ہے", with the alternative rite of reciting the saint's **نام نامی [OCR?]** and blowing — is the only
   present-tense shrine practice in the whole range, and **neither the saint nor the town is named on
   the page** (p. 150, folio 146).
8. **The four karamat of pp. 148–150** (the four-sons prophecy at محمد پور, the mock funeral at ظفر
   آباد, the qalandar struck dead, the آسیب mazar) are unattached to any person (pp. 149–150, folios
   145–146).
9. **The healing formula "دائیں طرف یا احد اور بائیں طرف یا صمد…" and the Timur warning** at p. 166
   (folio 162) belong to a saint the page does not name.
10. **Shaikh Fath Allah Lawaudhi has no mazar, no shrine town, no urs, no custodian and no silsila** —
    the entry carries no وفات-place and no مزار heading at all (pp. 151–152, folios 147–148).
11. **Shaikh Ahmad Abdul Haq Rudaulvi is given no mazar, no shrine town and no urs** anywhere in his six
    pages (pp. 169–174, folios 165–170), only a line of succession.
12. **Shaikh Ahmad Khattu's death year is not stated**; the "وفات" heading carries only the mazar at
    **سرکھیج[OCR?]** (p. 188, folio 184). **Do not infer a date** from the reigns of سلطان فیروز or
    سلطان مظفر named on the same page.
13. **Shams al-Din Turk's mazar** — the site of a bai'at and of a tabarruk transfer — **is never
    located**; the khanqah is at پانی پت and the shaykh walks there and back the same day, but the town
    of the mazar is not stated (p. 172, folio 168; p. 171, folio 167).
14. **The place where Shah Nematullah Wali first died and where the burial quarrel occurred is not
    stated** (p. 168, folio 164).
15. **How قنوج[OCR?], the plain designated by the sounding tank, relates to مکن پور, the residence and
    mazar**, is not stated (p. 177, folio 173).
16. **Shaikh Sarang's mazar is "لکھوء[OCR?] سے کچھ فاصلہ پر"** — the town flagged and the distance given
    only as "کچھ فاصلہ", some distance (p. 183, folio 179).
17. **The referent of "اپنے دادا" in the nazrana earmark.** Khwaja Husain Nagauri tells his son to build
    "حضرت خواجہ بزرگ اور **اپنے دادا** کے روضہ" and the sum is then spent on the mazars of "حضرت خواجہ
    غریب نواز اور حضرت صوفی حمیدالدین ناگوری" (p. 206, folio 202). The son's grandfather would be Khwaja
    Husain's own father, whom the range never names; حمیدالدین ناگوری is introduced as a forebear whose
    اولاد he is, not as a grandfather (p. 205, folio 201). **Either "دادا" is used loosely or a
    generation is missing; do not state a genealogy from this sentence.**
18. **حاشیہ 4 of باب 34 supports nothing identifiable.** The حواشی block lists four items (p. 194,
    folio 190) but the text carries markers only to "3" (pp. 192–193, folios 188–189), so "اخبار الاخیار
    (اردو ترجمہ) ص 329" cannot be attached — most likely to the کرامات of pp. 193–194 (folios 189–190),
    but the page must be re-read before the الف/ب story is credited to اخبار الاخیار rather than to the
    book.

#### Internal contradictions, kept side by side

- **Rukn al-Din's father is given two different names on adjacent pages**: the rubric "**رکن الدین
  [OCR?]**" (p. 113, folio 109) against the narrative's **حضرت شیخ صدر الدین عارف**, twice (p. 114,
  folio 110). The rubric reading is OCR-flagged; the narrative reading is not. **Do not carry the rubric
  value without this note.**
- **Shah Nematullah Wali's death is stated twice and the two statements sit awkwardly.** Under "وفات":
  "آپ نے 884ھ میں وفات پائی۔ مزار پر انوار دہکلی[OCR?] میں واقع ہے" (p. 167, folio 163). Under
  "کرامات", after his وصال and the burial quarrel, he sits up, declares "ہم یہاں نہیں مرتے" and goes to
  دہکلی[OCR?] to die (p. 168, folio 164). **The book does not reconcile the two accounts.**
- **The Deccan appears only in the second telling of Ahmad Khan's dream.** The dream as first told
  promises "اس کی سلطنت کی بشارت"; the recognition scene says the elder "اس کو **دکن** کی سلطنت کی بشارت
  دی تھی" (p. 168, folio 164). Neither is flagged; do not harmonise them.
- **The Delhi healing is told twice with a one-letter difference that changes its sense**: "ان کی
  **صحبت** کے لئے دعا فرمائی" (p. 129, folio 125) against "آپ نے ان کی **صحت** کے لئے دعا فرمائی"
  (p. 131, folio 127). **Neither word is OCR-flagged.** Quote whichever page you cite.
- **A doctrinal passage that both forbids and prescribes the same three practices.** Under حبس دم:
  "جوگیوں کی ہر ایک بات سے پرہیز رکھے اور وہ تیوں چیزیں یہ ہیں۔ حبس دم نشست مخصوص اور **ظفر تکیہ[OCR?]**۔
  یہ تینوں چیزیں سالک کے واسطے بہت ضروی [as printed] ہیں" (pp. 162–163, folios 158–159). **Do not quote
  this passage as doctrine without re-reading the page image**; the negation may have been lost in print
  or in transcription.
- **An apparent inversion in the patch simile.** The passage first says the true murid "جو پیر کے کہنے
  پر چلتا ہے" is the white patch, then says "جو شخص کہ پیر کے کہنے پر چلے وہ **رسمی مرید** ہے", the black
  patch (p. 199, folio 195). **The two clauses give opposite senses to the same condition and neither is
  OCR-flagged.** Quote only the white-patch and black-patch images, not the qualifying clause.
- **The printed Urdu translation of Nizam al-Din's Persian letter does not match the printed Persian.**
  The Persian reads "طریق دفع دنیا **قناعت** و است طریق دفع نفس و شیطاں **التجا کردن بحق** ساقہ فسالہ
  [OCR?]" (pp. 121–122, folios 117–118) — contentment against the world, petition to God against the nafs
  and Satan — while the Urdu "ترجمہ" reads "دنیا کو دفع کرنے کا طریقہ خداوند تعالٰی سے التجا کرنا ہے"
  (p. 122, folio 118), dropping **قناعت** and moving the petition onto the world; the translation also
  ends before the Persian does. **Do not quote the Urdu gloss as the letter's sense.**
- **A son's nisba printed two ways on one page.** Gesu Daraz's second son is "سید محمد اصغر
  **حسنی[OCR?]**" under شادی اور اولاد and in the khalifa list, but "سید محمد اصغر **حسینی**" in the
  sajjada-nashini sentence, **all on p. 156 (folio 152)**. The flagged form is the one that appears
  twice. **Record both; prefer neither.**
- **Khuldabad and Daulatabad are used without explanation.** The father moves the household to **دولت
  آباد** (p. 154, folio 150), the mother is then said to have stayed after his death at **خلد آباد**,
  and the education section places the boy's first exoteric studies at **خلد آباد** (pp. 154–155, folios
  150–151). **The book never says whether the two names are one place or two. Record each as printed.**
- **Shah Madar's stated birth and death years are very far apart and the book states no age.** Birth
  "یکم شوال 442ھ" (p. 175, folio 171); death "17 جمادی الاول 838ھ" (p. 177, folio 173); the only comment
  is "آپ کی عمر بہت ہوئی" (p. 178, folio 174). **Both years are recorded exactly as printed and no
  arithmetic or conversion is offered.**
- **One death year sits outside the book's own ascending order.** The chapters run 848, [باب 31 not
  stated], 857ھ, 864ھ, 870ھ, 880ھ, 882ھ, then **822ھ** for باب 37 (p. 198, folio 194), then 896ھ, 901ھ,
  921ھ. The 822ھ is **not OCR-flagged**; it is the one date in chunk 010 a human should check against the
  page image.
- **The children of Ahmad Abdul Haq are described in a sentence that will not parse.** "بڑے صاحب زادے
  شیخ عزیز اور دوسرے صاحب زادے پیدا ہونے کے چند روز بعد فوت ہوئے، آپ کے تیسرے صاحب زادے شیخ عارف صاحب
  سلسلہ ہوئے" (p. 172, folio 168) — read literally, the eldest and the second both died a few days after
  birth, which leaves the naming of the eldest odd. Not flagged; the heading above it is printed
  "شادی اور اولا" for "اولاد", also unflagged.
- **A sentence whose travel sequence contradicts itself, and which ends in an unopened quotation mark.**
  "بعد ازاں **کاظمین[OCR?]**' بغداد' نجف اشرف ہوتے ہوئے پھر ہندوستان تشریف لائے اور بغداد میں حضرت غوث
  الاعظم سے ملاقات کی“" (p. 176, folio 172, حاشیہ 5) — as printed the meeting in Baghdad falls **after**
  the arrival in India. **Do not state where or when Shah Madar met Ghaus al-Azam without re-reading this
  page.**
- **A sentence with "والد" twice and no clear subject.** "آپ کے والد کو حضرت شیخ رکن الدین ابو الفتح رحمۃ
  اللہ علیہ کا یہ پیغام اپنے والد ماجد کو پہنچایا" (p. 134, folio 130) — who conveyed the message to whom
  cannot be read off the sentence. The outcome is clear; the courier is not.
- **A list of three that names two.** "وہ تین علم یعنی شریعت' و حقیقت کا عالم ہو" (p. 139, folio 135) —
  three sciences announced and only شریعت and حقیقت named, طریقت apparently dropped. **Not OCR-flagged.
  Quote it as printed.**
- **A duplicated clause in the شریعت/طریقت/حقیقت passage.** "اور جسم کا معصیت سے شریعت ہے' اور دل کا نگاہ
  رکھنا کدورت بشریت سے طریقت ہے۔ اور جسم کا معصیت سے شریعت ہے' اور دل کا نگاہ **رکھا** کدورت بشریت سے
  طریقت ہے" (p. 139, folio 135) — printed twice, the second time with "رکھا" for "رکھنا", and not flagged.
  **Do not quote this passage as a continuous text.**
- **A doubled word inside the stone karamat.** "پتھر کی صفت بھی اس میں ہے **لوا[OCR?]** بھی لوہا لکڑی
  بھی" (p. 190, folio 186) lists iron twice, once flagged and once not, where the question asked names
  three materials. **Quote the question, not this answer.**
- **A hadith printed in garbled Arabic with a gloss that covers only part of it.** "وانصفوا بصفات اللّٰہ
  یا تخلقو ابا خلاق اللّٰہ او کما قال", glossed "یعنی خدا کی عادت اور صفت کے ساتھ تم کو اپنی عادت کرنی
  چاہیے" (p. 180, folio 176). The word-division is plainly wrong ("تخلقو ابا خلاق") and **the passage is
  not OCR-flagged**, so the fault may be in the print. Quote both exactly as printed.
- **The printed age at death agrees with the printed years and is the book's own figure**, not a
  computation: birth "4 رجب 720ھ" (p. 153, folio 149), death "16 ذیقعدہ 825ھ" and "بوقت وفات آپ کی عمر
  ایک سو پانچ سال کی تھی" (p. 156, folio 152). Note that **the same number, 105, is also given for the
  count of his books** (p. 158, folio 154) — a coincidence in the print, not an inference.
- **An age with nothing in this range to check it against.** "(170) ایک سو ستر سال سے زیادہ" (p. 130,
  folio 126) is stated with **no birth year on those pages**; the birth date must be sought in chunk 006
  before any arithmetic, and the book itself does none.
- **Two Rajab 736 AH dates twelve days apart**, both printed and neither flagged: the family reaches
  Delhi "4، رجب 736ھ" (p. 154, folio 150) and the bai'at is "16، رجب 736ھ" (p. 155, folio 151).
- **The costly dress and the concealment stand in the same sentence-run and the book offers no
  reconciliation**: Shah Alam "اکثر قیمتی لباس زیب تن فرماتے تھے" and "آپ نے لوگوں سے اپنے آپ کو پوشیدہ
  رکھنے کی کوشش کی"، "مشرب آپ کا ملامتیہ تھا" (p. 195, folio 191).
- **Two entries carry the same laqab-explanation problem in reverse**: باب 34 spends a page on
  "لقب کی وجہ تسمیہ" (p. 192, folio 188) while باب 40 lists "القاب" and offers nothing (p. 208,
  folio 204).

#### Print oddities that are not OCR-flagged, recorded as printed

- "چار قرص" becomes "چار **قرض**" inside the same karamat — four loaves, then four loans (p. 119,
  folio 115). The transcription does not flag it, so it may be the print's own error; **treat the ration
  as four قرص and check the page.**
- "خلفائے **راشیدین**" for راشدین (p. 148, folio 144); "**نمازہ** جنازہ" (p. 150, folio 146); "یہ بھی
  کہا **جاتا**" without ہے (p. 150, folio 146); "**ضروی**" for ضروری (p. 163, folio 159); "کشف **پر**
  گا" for پڑے گا (p. 165, folio 161); "شادی اور **اولا**" for اولاد (p. 172, folio 168); "**کئی** کہتا
  ہے" where کوئی is expected (p. 200, folio 196); "تخت سے دست **برادری**" for دست برداری (p. 146,
  folio 142); "یا دوست میں مشغول ہو یا دوست کی یاد **ہیں**", where the second clause does not parse
  (p. 163, folio 159); "درد و رماں" and "درماں"/"حرمان" alternating in the sixteenth condition (p. 162,
  folio 158).
- **Spelling variants worth recording before quoting**: "سلطان فیروز شاہ" against "سلطان فیروز تغلق"
  (pp. 129, 137; folios 125, 133); "شیخ علاء الدین بنگالی" against "شیخ علاؤ الدین بنگالی" on one page
  (p. 146, folio 142); "جہانگیر" against "جہاں گیر" (pp. 145, 146; folios 141, 142); "شیخ حاجی چراغ ہند
  سہروردی" against "شیخ چراغ ہندی" (pp. 146–147, folios 142–143); "اوچہ / اونچہ / اوچ" (pp. 133, 134,
  146; folios 129, 130, 142); "قوم الدین" against "قوام الدین" in consecutive sentences (p. 191,
  folio 187); "معارج الولایت" against "معراج الولایت" (pp. 111, 169; folios 107, 165);
  "ذوالفقار بدیع" / "ذو والفقار بدیع" / "ذو الفقار بدیع" (pp. 180–181, folios 176–177).
- **An unflagged word of uncertain sense in an austerity statement**: Shaikh Ahmad Khattu "روزے رکھتے
  اور **کھل** کے ٹکڑے سے افطار کرتے" (p. 187, folio 183) — recorded as printed rather than interpreted.
- **An unattributed and unclosed quotation at the end of باب 37**: "ایک دن آپ نے سنا کہ کئی کہتا ہے کہ :
  'خوب پڑھتے ہو' جیسا کہ پڑھنا چاہیے ویسے ہی پڑھتے ہو۔" (p. 200, folio 196) — the closing quotation mark
  is absent and **the speaker is unidentified**.

#### Footnote-apparatus problems

- **باب 25 has fifteen footnote markers and no حواشی block within chunk 008's pages** — markers 1–15 all
  appear in the text (pp. 153–165, folios 149–161) **but the block they point to does not**. **No
  statement in باب 25 can be attributed to a named earlier work from those pages** — including the death
  date, the Gulbarga mazar and the dome. (If the p. 166 block is that entry's apparatus, see the seam
  note above, this changes; it must be confirmed.)
- **Markers 1–3 of the unnamed entry's حواشی fall before p. 148.** The block lists nine items (p. 150,
  folio 146) but only markers 4–9 appear in the transcribed text, so "لطائف اشرفی فی بیان طوائف صوفی
  (فارسی) ص 990", "اخبار الاخیار (اردو ترجمہ) ص 446" and "تذکرۃ العابدین ص 262" cannot be attached to
  any statement in this range.
- **باب 33 has no footnote marker and no حواشی block at all** (p. 191, folio 187). Its death date
  "25 رمضان 864ھ" and the statement that the mazar at بہرائچ lies near مولوی شاہ نعیم اللہ's mazar stand
  **on the compiler's authority alone.**
- **Identical citations supporting different statements**: حواشی 2 and 3 of Shah Madar's block are both
  "مدار اعظم ص 29،34" (p. 181, folio 177).
- **The same page number given for a Persian original and its Urdu translation**: حاشیہ 2 cites "اخبار
  الاخیار (اردو ترجمہ) ص 384" and حاشیہ 4 "اخبار الاخیار (فارسی) ص 384" (p. 174, folio 170) — one is
  probably a slip.
- **Truncated, unclosed and run-together citations**: "اسرار”" with a stray closing quotation mark
  (p. 166, folio 162); "انوار العارفین (فارسی ص 457" with an unclosed parenthesis and a حاشیہ running
  two citations together (p. 184, folio 180); "اخبار الاخیار (اردو ترجمہ ص 332" (p. 189, folio 185);
  "اخبار الاخیار (اردو ترجمہ 234" **with no "ص"** (p. 190, folio 186); "سیر العارفین سالک السالکین (جلد
  دوم) ص 427" — two works, one page number — and حواشی 6 and 12 reading "سیر العارفین" with no page at
  all (p. 142, folio 138).
- **Three chapters give a death year with no month or day and no source**: Fakhr al-Din Maruzi "736ھ"
  (p. 122, folio 118), Husam al-Din Sokhta "741ھ" (p. 124, folio 120) and Sadr al-Din Ahmad "759ھ"
  (p. 126, folio 122) — **none footnoted, unlike Rukn al-Din's.**

#### Passages that must not be quoted as texts

- The **حصہ چہارم** couplet, three flags in two lines (p. 143, folio 139), and the **حصہ پنجم** couplet,
  flagged at its last word (p. 203, folio 199).
- The **unnamed saint's Persian ghazal**, five flags (p. 148, folio 144).
- **Gesu Daraz's specimen verses**, four flags (p. 159, folio 155).
- **Shah Mina's ruba'i**, five flags in two lines, and **باب 30's قطعہ**, two flags (pp. 193, 185;
  folios 189, 181).
- The **Nizami couplet** quoted only to be dismissed (p. 173, folio 169).
- **Husam al-Din Manikpuri's involuntary Persian line**, flagged at its tail and given **no Urdu gloss**
  (p. 200, folio 196).
- The **Quranic quotation in the Friday wazifa**, flagged mid-verse (p. 164, folio 160).
- The **Urdu "ترجمہ" of Nizam al-Din's Persian letter**, which does not render its Persian (p. 122,
  folio 118).
- The **حبس دم passage**, which forbids and prescribes the same three items (pp. 162–163, folios
  158–159).
- The **شریعت/طریقت/حقیقت passage at p. 139 (folio 135)**, whose clause is printed twice.
- The **saying "مفتیان کی جڑ اللہ شہ کمال نے پٹی"[OCR?]**, printed with a flag and **no gloss**; "شہ" and
  the syntax of "اللہ" cannot be resolved (p. 210, folio 206). Quote as printed; do not paraphrase.
- The **green-grass exchange**, both sides flagged (p. 170, folio 166).
- The **two-murshid consequence clause** (p. 160, folio 156).
- The **hadith at p. 180 (folio 176)**, whose word-division is wrong and whose gloss covers only part.

#### Lists that must not be counted or enumerated

- **Gesu Daraz's khalifa list runs names together after "شیخ صدر الدین"** and carries no separators;
  whether "سید جمال الدین" is a separate khalifa or part of the preceding patronymic **cannot be settled
  from the transcription**, so the list's length is uncertain. "خونذیر[OCR?] (ایرچہ[OCR?])" is doubly
  flagged and may be a name, a place or both (p. 156, folio 152).
- **Shah Madar's sixteen-name khulafa list is unsafe to split.** Apostrophes fall in some places and not
  others, two words are flagged, and several entries are plainly two names run together ("سید صدر جہاں
  شیخ آدم صوفی", "میاں سیف اللہ شیخ فخر الدین") (p. 178, folio 174).

#### Provenance

- **No publisher's rights line, price line, digitisation stamp or library watermark appears anywhere in
  pp. 110–210.** All five chunk notes record the same negative for their own pages.
- **The only transcriber's scan-defect note in the range** is `[last line clipped at foot of scan]` at
  p. 206 (folio 202), which loses the head of Khwaja Husain Nagauri's book-list: p. 206 ends "آپ کی
  مشہور کتابیں حسب ذیل ہیں[OCR?]" and p. 207 (folio 203) resumes "نور النبی، آپ نے قسم ثالث[OCR?]
  مفتاح[OCR?] پر بھی شرح…". **Whether "نور النبی" is the first title or a continuation cannot be told
  from the transcription.**
- **Three pages in the range are `[blank page]`** — pp. 144, 202 and 204 — and print no folio marker, so
  **folios 140, 198 and 200 never appear**. The folio = PDF − 4 relation is otherwise unbroken across all
  101 pages.

### pp. 211–303 (chunks 011–015)

#### A. Placement doubts — facts and figures that could not be placed under an archive row

**Twenty-eight distinct items. None has been resolved; none may be resolved without the page images or
the adjoining chunks.**

1. **The unnamed Qadiri shaykh of کتھیل[OCR?] (pp. 211–213, folios 207–209) must not be mapped to
   `shrine-of-baba-shah-kamal`.** That row's location is "Muslim Town / Samanabad (near Ichhra),
   Lahore" and its figure "Hazrat Baba Shah Kamal", Qadiri; the chunk's only locational statement about
   its subject is "کتھیل[OCR?]" (pp. 211, 212; folios 207, 208), and it gives him **no death year and
   no mazar at all**. The silsila agreement is not enough. Inside the quoted words of Mujaddid Alf-e
   Sani he is called "شاہ صاحب" (p. 211, folio 207), and the same quotation names "حضرت شاہ کمال قادری
   قدس سرہ" in a chain of transmission — **but the chunk never states that the two are the same
   person**, and no name is printed as the entry's own. **Attribute nothing on pp. 211–213 to a named
   saint until the previous chunk is read.**
2. **حضرت شیخ طاہر بندگی (p. 211, folio 207) resembles `tahir-bandagi-qadri` and is left unmapped.**
   That row is "Darbar Hazrat Tahir Bandagi Qadri", Miani Sahib Graveyard, Lahore, died "8 Muharram
   1040 AH / 8 August 1630 CE", Qadri. The book's figure appears **once**, as one of four who received
   "جلاؤ بقاء[OCR?]", with **no town, no district, no pir, no death date and no silsila of his own**.
3. **"شاہ یوسف غوث بھکری" in the p. 211 (folio 207) name list is not `shah-yousuf`.** That row is
   "Shah Yousuf, Shahpur, Punjab"; the book's nisba as printed is "بھکری", and the name stands in an
   undifferentiated list with no dates, no shrine and no silsila.
4. **باب 41's "حضرت شیخ بہاء الدین" (p. 214, folio 210) is not the mapped Bahauddin Zakariya.** The book
   gives باب 41's subject a Qadiri silsila and Shattari mashrab, a father "ابراہیم" son of "عطاءاللہ
   انصاری", a home town "قصبہ جنید[OCR?]" and a death in "961ھ", while "حضرت بہاء الدین زکریا ملتانی"
   appears separately at p. 222 (folio 218). **Two distinct figures sharing a given name inside one
   chunk; a human checking the mapping must not merge them.**
5. **"خواجہ غریب نواز" must NOT be mapped to `khawaja-feroz-ud-din-gharib-nawaz`, at any of its five
   occurrences in the range.** That row is "Darbar Hazrat Khawaja Feroz-ud-Din Gharib Nawaz Chishti
   Nizami", Miani Sahib Graveyard, Lahore, died "November 27, 1981 (Friday)". The book's figure is a
   darbar at **اجمیر** in which a saint who died in "927ھ" resided seventy years (pp. 217, 219;
   folios 213, 215); the mazar an Ajmer visitor attends (p. 234, folio 230); "حضرت خواجہ معین الدین
   چشتی اجمیری" (p. 285, folio 281); and an unlocated authority commanding a bai'at and a marriage
   (p. 286, folio 282), where he is given **no town, no shrine, no silsila and no dates at all**. **A
   different person and a different place carrying the same title.**
6. **حضرت شاہ عبدالرزاق جھنجھانہ must NOT be mapped to `shrine-of-baba-shah-chiragh`, and the near-miss
   is sharp.** That row's figure is "Syed Abdul Razzaq (Shah Chiragh)", "The Mall (near Lahore High
   Court), Lahore", died 1657, **Qadiri** — and the book's saint is named "شاہ عبدالرزاق" and is
   "قادریہ سلسلہ سے وابستہ" (p. 232, folio 228). **But the book places his mazar at "جھنجھانہ", not
   Lahore, gives his death as "949ھ", and attaches to him a nisba the row does not carry and an
   associate at "پانی پت"** (p. 232, folio 228). **Do not let the name-plus-silsila coincidence close
   this identification.**
7. **حضرت بابا فرید الدین گنج شکر (p. 236, folio 232) is not mapped.** He appears only as the ancestor
   of Shaikh Salim Chishti and the last link of a genealogy; the chunk gives him **no mazar, no town, no
   death year and no silsila**, and the only town it associates with the family is **اجودھن**, never
   Pakpattan. The identification with `shrine-of-fariduddin-ganjshakar` rests on the name alone.
8. **"شیخ رکن الدین" in Shaikh Salim Chishti's khalifa list (p. 239, folio 235)**, cf.
   `shrine-of-shah-rukn-e-alam` (Multan) — a name in a list with no location, date, pir or silsila. Not
   mapped.
9. **"شیخ کمال الدین" in the same list (p. 239, folio 235)**, cf. `shrine-of-baba-shah-kamal` (Lahore) —
   the standing شاہ کمال warning applies. Not mapped.
10. **"شیخ موسیٰ" in Shaikh Jalal al-Din's khalifa list (p. 243, folio 239)** is also the name of Salim
    Chishti's elder brother (p. 236, folio 232); **the two are not necessarily one man** and the chunk
    does not say.
11. **"شاہ بلاول" and "شاہ بلاول دکنی" in the sixteen-khalifa list (p. 254, folio 250) are NOT to be
    mapped** to `shah-noorani-shrine-syed-bilawal-shah-noorani` (Lahoot Lamakan valley, Khuzdar
    District, Balochistan; "Syed Bilawal Shah Noorani"). They are two of four title-bearers in a Lahore
    khalifa list, with **no location, no date and no silsila**.
12. **"مولانا شیخ طاہر لاہوری" (p. 265, folio 261) is NOT to be mapped** to `tahir-bandagi-qadri`. He
    resembles the row by personal name and city but the chunk gives him nothing but a place in a khalifa
    list — **no mazar, no death year** — and places him among the khalifas of a Naqshbandi shaykh where
    the row records Qadri.
13. **حضرت شاہ کمال کتھیلی[OCR?] (pp. 260–262, folios 256–258) is NOT `shrine-of-baba-shah-kamal`.** The
    nisba is printed "کتھیلی[OCR?]" twice with a flag and once as "حضرت کمال کتھیلی"; he is given **no
    mazar, no town, no death year**, and appears as a Qadiriya pir in the Sirhind/Punjab plains.
14. **حضرت میاں میر's own entry, باب 53 (p. 269, folio 265), is left unmapped by chunk 013, while chunk
    015 maps the same name from pp. 292–294 (folios 288–290). This is an unreconciled disagreement
    between two chunk workers inside one range and is the first thing a human should settle.** باب 53
    gives the name "میر محمد", the laqabs "میاں میر، شاہ میر، میاں جیو، بالا پیر", and a birth at
    "سیوستان… جو ٹھٹھ اور بھکر کے مابین واقع ہے" in "938ھ" — which agrees with the row's "of Sind" — but
    **states no shrine town, no death year, no silsila and no pir**, the entry breaking off immediately
    after the القاب heading. باب 56 gives a Lahore murshid called "حضرت میاں میر", a Qadiri line at one
    remove, and **no personal name, no Sindhi origin, no mazar and no death year**. **Neither body of
    material contains what the other lacks, and the book never joins them.**
15. **The unnamed Lahore saint of pp. 270–277 (folios 266–273) is not mapped.** He resembles
    `shrine-of-mian-mir` (row: "Alam Ganj (Dharampura), Lahore, Punjab, Pakistan"; figure "Mian Mir (Mir
    Mohammad of Sind)"; died 1635; Qadiri) on three of the book's own statements — mazar "لاہور میں"
    (p. 273, folio 269), the Qadiri affiliation carried through Khizr, "سلسلہ عالیہ قادریہ" (p. 270,
    folio 266), and the Sindh connection implied by "سیوستان کے پہاڑوں" (p. 270, folio 266). **Against
    the mapping**: the pages print **no name at all**; the locality is **باغبانوں کے محلے** (p. 271,
    folio 267), not Alam Ganj / Dharampura; and the death date "17 ربیع الاول 1045ھ" (p. 273,
    folio 269) cannot be set beside the row's "1635" without a calendar conversion, **which this pass
    does not perform**. The only appellation anywhere in those pages is inside reported speech —
    Shah Jahan's "حضرت میاں جیو جیسا کوئی درویش نہیں دیکھا" (pp. 272–273, folios 268–269) — and the
    entry twice makes his name a narrative object without printing it (p. 271, folio 267; p. 272,
    folio 268).
16. **"باغبانوں کے محلے" (p. 271, folio 267) is a second trap on the same page and must not be read as a
    match either.** The quarter of the gardeners is the locality of `madho-lal-hussain` ("Baghbanpura
    (near Shalimar Gardens), Lahore"), whose figure is Shah Hussain, died 1599, Malamati — **nothing in
    that entry corresponds**: a different pir, a different silsila, a different death year and a
    different Lahore burial statement.
17. **حضرت شاہ بلاول (باب 54, pp. 278–280, folios 274–276) is not mapped; the resemblance is name-only.**
    "آپ کا نام سید بلاول ہے" (p. 278, folio 274) matches the personal name of
    `shah-noorani-shrine-syed-bilawal-shah-noorani`. **Everything locational contradicts it**: forebears
    settled at **شیخو پورہ**, birth there in "976ھ", study at **لاہور**, and a mazar "اسی موضع میں لاہو
    [OCR?] کے قریب… جس میں آپ رہتے تھے" (pp. 278–279, folios 274–275). **A bare name match across the
    subcontinent.** His shrine town rests on an OCR-flagged word one letter short of لاہور and on an
    **unnamed موضع**; his **silsila is never named**, only a three-link chain — شیخ شمس الدین ← شیخ ابو
    اسحاق ← شیخ داؤد جہنیوال [OCR?] (p. 278, folio 274), the last flagged.
18. **The `data-darbar` mapping rests on the figure's name, not on a printed town.** The book names the
    shrine four times as "مزار" of "حضرت داتا گنج بخش" and has the figure there say "میں علی ہجویری
    ہوں" (pp. 250–251, folios 246–247) — both designations matching the row's principal figure element
    for element. **But the chunk never prints a town beside that mazar.** The Lahore placement is
    contextual only (p. 249, folio 245; pp. 250–251, folios 246–247; pp. 251–252, folios 247–248). **A
    human should confirm that the book intends the Lahore shrine**; a second Data Ganj Bakhsh elsewhere
    is not raised by the text but is not excluded by it either.
19. **The `shergarh` mapping is thin and may have to be withdrawn.** The book places حضرت شیخ داؤد at
    **شیر گڑھ** (p. 256, folio 252), the row's own town, and makes him Abul Muali's pir — which the
    archive's row for Abul Muali independently records as "Affiliation with Syed Dawood Bandgi Kirmani".
    **But the chunk never gives him the nisba "بندگی" or "کرمانی", never states his mazar, his death
    year or his silsila-name**, and its one dating of his death is the flagged and impossible
    "1110ھ[OCR?]" (p. 257, folio 253). The row's "1575" and "Qadiri" are neither confirmed nor
    contradicted. **If a human judges the pir-agreement insufficient, this mapping should be withdrawn.**
20. **The `darbar-abul-muali-qadri` row's figure-name and the book's name do not line up exactly.** The
    row reads "Hazrat Syed Muhammad Khair ul Deen, known as Shah Abul Muali Qadri"; the book gives the
    **name** as "المعالی" and "شاہ خیر الدین" only as a **laqab** beside "اسد الدین" (p. 255,
    folio 251), and **never uses the word "قادری" of him at all** — his silsila is unnamed, his
    attachment to Abd al-Qadir Jilani is "اویسی" (p. 255, folio 251) and his bai'at is to Shaikh Dawud
    (p. 256, folio 252). Both readings are recorded; neither has been harmonised.
21. **The `madho-lal-hussain` mapping of pp. 253–254 (folios 249–250) rests on statements, not on a
    printed name.** Page 253 begins inside a quoted argument with a qazi and carries no chapter number
    and no name line; the subject is "آپ" throughout, and the only proper name is "حسین" inside the
    quoted words at the top of the page. The mapping rests on four agreements — mazar in لاہور, the
    annual "میلہ چراغاں", "حضرت مادھو" as sajjada nashin, and "ملامتیہ طریقہ" — **and a human should
    still confirm the name and chapter number from the preceding chunk.**
22. **"حضرت میر سید کالپوری" (p. 286, folio 282) and "حضرت میرسید محمد کالپوی" (p. 303, folio 299) may
    or may not be one man.** The nisba is spelled **کالپوری** in one place and **کالپوی** in the other,
    and the personal name "محمد" appears only in the second. **Neither is OCR-flagged. Do not merge them
    without re-reading both pages.**
23. **The unnamed Agra dervish of باب 56 must not be identified.** "آگرہ پہنچ کر آپ اس شخص کے ہمراہ ان
    بزرگ کے پاس گئے' لیکن آپ کی تسکین نہیں ہوئی" (p. 293, folio 289). The book does not name him, and
    the range separately names **two** Agra elders of the period (p. 303, folio 299). **Do not fill this
    in from either.**
24. **حضرت ملا شاہ (باب 56) has no archive row and is the strongest new-row candidate the range
    produces**: a dated Lahore mazar ("1069ھ"; "مزار پر انوار لاہور میں واقع ہے", p. 294, folio 290), a
    stated Qadiri affiliation, a named pir already in the archive, a named khalifa (Dara Shikoh), a
    diwan, and a Badakhshan origin — **none of which the 169 rows carry**.
25. **حضرت سرمد شہید has no row and must not be mapped to one.** His mazar is "دہلی جامع مسجد کے نیچے"
    (p. 300, folio 296) — India. The Pakistani places in his entry (**سندھ**, **ٹھٹھہ**) are a trade
    route and a love episode, not a shrine (pp. 297–298, folios 293–294).
26. **The Delhi mazars of قطب الدین بختیار کاکی and نظام الدین اولیاء (p. 285, folio 281) have no
    archive row** and are recorded only as stops on an itinerary.
27. **"شیخ صدر الدین", met at Multan, has no archive row and cannot be proposed as one from these
    pages** (p. 222, folio 218): the chunk gives him only the name and the meeting — no mazar, no date,
    no silsila.
28. **The book makes حضرت خضر علیہ السلام[OCR?] a living, locatable Qadiri shaykh** with an oven in the
    سیوستان mountains and a twelve-month residence there, who takes a bai'at and grants a khirqa
    (p. 270, folio 266) and later visits a sickbed alongside Abd al-Qadir Jilani (p. 271, folio 267).
    His name is OCR-flagged at first occurrence. **This is the book's claim about a saint's initiatic
    chain; it is recorded, not resolved, and it cannot be placed.**

#### B. Entries cut by chunk boundaries — what is outside the range

- **The range opens mid-entry** (pp. 211–213, folios 207–209) and its subject is never named: a tail of
  a name list, then "سیرت پاک", "ارشادات", "کشف و کرامات" and a five-item حواشی block, with **no chapter
  heading and no name**; the next heading, "باب 41", appears only at p. 214 (folio 210).
- **باب 45's footnote block is cut off** (p. 230, folio 226): only حواشی 1–2 are printed while the text
  carries markers up to **14**. **The sources for the birth year "861ھ", the Uwaisi fayz, the bisharat,
  the Shahabad residence, the Gangoh migration, the seven sons, both death years, the cultivation
  statement, the hunger teachings and both karamat fall outside the chunk. Do not attribute any of them
  to سفینۃ الاولیاء or سیر الاقطاب.**
- **p. 231 (folio 227) carries only a حواشی block, numbered 3 to 14, with no heading and no name.**
  حواشی 1–2 of that entry and the entire entry they support fall outside the range. **Attribute nothing
  on p. 231 to any saint.**
- **باب 50 is split across two chunks.** Chunk 012 ends mid-sentence inside Hussain's division of the
  five pillars (p. 252, folio 248); his death date, mazar, urs, khalifas and the qazi's reply are all
  outside that chunk and are supplied by chunk 013 (pp. 253–254, folios 249–250).
- **باب 53 breaks off after "القاب"** with the redundant printing "کے القاب کے القاب سے پکارے جاتے ہیں"
  (p. 269, folio 265). **Mian Mir's وفات, مزار, silsila and pir all fall outside the range**, and باب
  53's four footnote markers have no حواشی block anywhere in it.
- **باب 55 breaks off** at Gharib Nawaz's instruction about bai'at and marriage (p. 285, folio 281).
  **Abu al-Ala's death date, his mazar, his silsila, his khalifas and the whole of باب 55's حواشی block
  fall outside chunk 014**, though markers 2؎ through 7؎ (and a probable 1؎) appear in the text. Some
  of this is supplied by chunk 015's opening entry — **but chunk 015 records that entry's chapter number
  and heading as outside its own pages too, and does not itself assert the two are one entry**
  (p. 286, folio 282; p. 290, folio 286; p. 291, folio 287; p. 303, folio 299). A human should confirm
  the join.
- **The range closes mid-sentence.** باب 58 breaks off at "کہار آپ کو بجائے حضرت نعمان کی خانقاہ پر لے
  جانے کے حضرت امیر ابو" (p. 303, folio 299). **Nothing here is the outcome of the palanquin episode**,
  and the entry's حواشی (marker 1 appears in the text) fall outside.

#### C. Internal contradictions and numbers that cannot stand

- **Two years and an age for one saint, from two chunks.** "آپ 945ھ میں لاہور میں پیدا ہوئے" (p. 249,
  folio 245) and "آپ نے 1008ھ میں وفات پائی، بوقت وفات آپ کی عمر ترسیٹھ[OCR?] سال کی تھی" (p. 253,
  folio 249). Chunk 013 records that its own pages give no birth date, so the age could not be checked
  there. **The age numeral is OCR-flagged. No arithmetic has been performed here and none should be
  until the flag is cleared against the page image.**
- **The khalifa arithmetic does not close.** "آپ کے سولہ خلیفہ تھے" — sixteen khalifas — but the named
  groups are four شاہ غریب (all unnamed) + four دیوان + **three** خاکی + four بلاول = **fifteen**
  (pp. 253–254, folios 249–250). **The numerals are not OCR-flagged.** Either a name is missing from the
  خاکی group or the total is wrong. The بلاول group is also introduced in the present tense ("کہلاتے
  ہیں") where the other three use the past.
- **An impossible settlement year inside باب 51.** Abul Muali came to Lahore "1110ھ[OCR?] میں اپنے پیر
  دستگیر کے وصال کے بعد" (p. 257, folio 253), yet died "16- ربیع الاول 1024ھ" (p. 257, folio 253) —
  **the settlement year stands eighty-six lunar years after the death year**. The settlement year is
  flagged; the death year is not. **Until the page is re-read from the PDF, only the death date should
  be cited.**
- **Neither of باب 51's two birth years can stand with its death year, and the book states no age.**
  910ھ and 920ھ against 1024ھ (p. 255, folio 251; p. 257, folio 253), with Delhi reached in 980ھ
  (p. 256, folio 252). **Recorded as printed; no age computed.**
- **باب 52's birth year is garbled and must not be repaired.** "سرہند میں آپ نے جمعہ، 14 شوال 1971ھ[OCR?]
  کو" (p. 260, folio 256) cannot stand as a Hijri date beside the death "28 صفر 1034ھ" (p. 265,
  folio 261). **Quote it as printed with its flag; do not reconstruct a year from the weekday, the month
  or the death date.**
- **The father's death year in باب 52 is likewise impossible.** "17 رجب 100ھ[OCR?] کو بعمر اسی سال"
  (p. 262, folio 258), while the son leaves Sirhind for the hajj "اپنے والد ماجد کے وصال کے بعد… 1008ھ
  میں" (p. 262, folio 258). Flagged; leave as printed.
- **Abdul Quddus's death date is doubly uncertain on the page.** The day-number is flagged — "آپ
  تیس[OCR?] جمادی الاخر 944ھ کو" — and a second year follows, "بعض نے آپ کی وفات 945ھ میں ہونا لکھا ہے"
  (p. 228, folio 224, حواشی 9–10). **Neither year should be preferred, and the day must carry its flag
  wherever it is quoted.** The book states no age and no birth-to-death arithmetic.
- **Two birth years for Shaikh Salim Chishti cannot both stand**: "884ھ" and "897ھ" (p. 236, folio 232),
  against hajj "931ھ", second departure "962ھ", return "976ھ", death "29 رمضان 979ھ" (pp. 236–238,
  folios 232–234). **The book states no age at death and no age at any journey.**
- **Internal arithmetic on Shaikh Ahmad Shaibani, for the record.** Ajmer at "اٹھارہ سال کی عمر",
  eighteen (p. 217, folio 213), a stay of "(70) ستر سال" (p. 217, folio 213), then Narnaul and Nagaur,
  dying "25 صفر 927ھ" (p. 218, folio 214). **The book states no age and no birth year; do not compute
  one.**
- **Spans that the book never relates to one another** for Madho Lal Hussain: twenty-six years in
  devotion (p. 250, folio 246), twelve years' attendance (p. 251, folio 247), sixteen years circling
  Madho's house (p. 252, folio 248) — **the book nowhere says how these sit together or when any began.**
  The father's conversion is placed only "سلطان فیروز شاہ کے عہد میں", with no year (p. 249, folio 245).
- **An age implying a birth year the book does not state.** Abu al-Ala's "71 سال" against a death of
  "9 صفر 1016ھ" (p. 286, folio 282) — **do not compute one.**
- **An internal chronology the book leaves open.** Dara Shikoh is Mulla Shah's "ممتاز خلیفہ" (p. 294,
  folio 290) and the addressee of his letters (p. 295, folio 291), while the same chunk narrates Dara's
  defeat (p. 298, folio 294) and dates Sarmad's execution to "1070ھ" (p. 300, folio 296), one year after
  Mulla Shah's stated death in "1069ھ" (p. 294, folio 290). **The book gives no date for Dara Shikoh and
  does not relate these facts. Do not build a sequence.**
- **Numbers to check against the page images.** "تیس [OCR?] سال" of service (p. 293, folio 289) is
  flagged while the "تیس سال" of sleeplessness (p. 294, folio 290) is not; "سات سال" of ذکر خفی
  (p. 294, folio 290), "تین سال" in Kashmir (p. 292, folio 288) and "تین سال" of being ignored (p. 293,
  folio 289) are unflagged; "(30) تیس[OCR?] سال" of residence at Shahabad is flagged (p. 228,
  folio 224).
- **No Hijri year anywhere in the range has been converted, and none should be.** The only CE values in
  play are the archive rows' ("1267", "1072", "1599", "1575", "1635", "1657", "1981", "8 August 1630"),
  and they are reported as row values, never merged with the book's.
- **The Mandu salutation passage contradicts itself.** "آپ نے بادشاہ کو اس طرح سلام کیا۔ اور فرمایا کہ
  اس طرح سلام کرنا اسلامی تعلیم کے خلاف ہے اور یہ طریقہ بدعت ہے" — he saluted the king in that manner
  and said that saluting in that manner is bid'at — followed immediately by "آپ جب بادشاہ کے سامنے گئے
  تو سلام علیکم کہا اور اس کے برابر بیٹھ گئے" (p. 219, folio 215). **A negative has probably been
  dropped; do not state that he did or did not perform the prostration without re-reading this page from
  the PDF.**
- **باب 42's descent statement is circular.** The chapter opens "حضرت شیخ محمد[OCR?] شیبانی کی اولاد سے
  ہیں" and then, under "خاندانی حالات", "آپ حضرت شیخ احمد محمد[OCR?] شیبانی کی اولاد سے ہیں" — making
  the subject a descendant of himself (p. 217, folio 213). **Either the ancestor's name or the subject's
  is misprinted; the flagged word is "محمد[OCR?]" in both. Do not build a lineage on this page.**
- **The two incompatible attitudes to the shrine, printed inside one entry.** The testament against a
  grave-form and a shop (p. 273, folio 269) against the اقوال on increased تصرف after death (p. 276,
  folio 272), under a mazar recorded as "مرجع خاص و عام" (p. 273, folio 269). **The book does not remark
  on it.**
- **The چراغاں contradiction across twelve folios.** The annual "میلہ چراغاں" reported "بڑی دھوم دھام
  سے" (p. 253, folio 249) and the same saint's fondness for "ناچ گانے" and use of "نشیلی چیزوں" (p. 254,
  folio 250), against "سماع، رقص، صندل اور چراغاں عرس کو منع فرماتے تھے" (p. 266, folio 262). **The
  compiler prints both and reconciles neither; this is the book's tension, not the transcriber's.**
- **A conflicting affiliation stated flatly and left unexplained**: Qadiri silsila and Shattari mashrab
  in one sentence, a purely Qadiri nisbat-statement, and a teaching section expounding "طریق شطاریہ" as
  the nearest road to God (p. 214, folio 210; pp. 215–216, folios 211–212).
- **The emperor of the court-and-prison passage is not named.** "سلاطین کے تعلقات" names Akbar and
  Jahangir (p. 264, folio 260), but the farman to the governor of Sirhind, the refusal of the sajda, the
  vizier's advice, the Gwalior internment and the release order are all attributed only to "بادشاہ"
  (p. 264, folio 260). **Do not attach that sequence to a named emperor.**
- **The rebel of Akbar's Thanesar halt is named "مزار حکیم" and the action "فرد کرنے"** (p. 243,
  folio 239); **neither word is flagged, and the rebel's identity must not be supplied from outside the
  page.**
- **Two designations for one Lahore antagonist.** "ایک دن قاضی لاہور نے آپ کو ڈھول پر ناچتے دیکھا۔ مخدوم
  الملک کو یہ بات ناگوار ہوئی" (p. 252, folio 248): the following sentence addresses "قاضی صاحب", so the
  book appears to use **قاضی لاہور** and **مخدوم الملک** of one person, **but it does not say so. Do not
  merge the two titles into one officeholder.**
- **Two names for one mazar in consecutive clauses.** Bahlol is said to have come "حضرت امام علی موسیٰ
  کے مزار سے" and to have been commanded "حضرت امام رضا کے مزار سے" in the very next clause (p. 249,
  folio 245). **Record both designations as printed.**
- **The Hindu faqir and Bawa Smel[OCR?] Puri are identified only by juxtaposition** (p. 212, folio 208):
  the episode opens "ایک ہندو فقیر" and, without a transition, calls the same man "باوا سمیل[OCR?] پوری"
  three lines later. **The identification is the book's, made by placement rather than by statement.**
- **Two sons of Ali, both printed "امام حسین".** "دو صاحب زادگان حضرت امام حسین [OCR?] رضی اللہ عنہ
  [OCR?] اور حضرت امام حسین رضی اللہ عنہ [OCR?] شہید کربلا" (p. 284, folio 280). The first name is
  flagged. **Do not supply the other name.** Only the second is identified as شہید کربلا.
- **A duplicated heading and block, with a parent named wrongly inside it.** "حسب و نسب" and its single
  sentence are printed **twice, identically**, at p. 281 (folio 277); within it, "آپ والدہ ماجد کی طرف
  سے حسینی اور والدہ ماجدہ کی طرف سے احراری ہیں" gives the paternal line under "والدہ ماجد" where "والد
  ماجد" is required by the contrast. **Neither the duplication nor the word is OCR-flagged. Do not
  assign حسینی to a parent without re-reading the page.**
- **A duplicated clause inside the موجودیت passage** (p. 267, folio 263): the same clause twice, the
  second time headed by a flagged "صرف[OCR?]" where the first has "معبود حقیقی". **Do not quote this
  passage as a text.**
- **A khalifa list that may contain one name or two.** "شیخ عبدالکبیر' شیخ جلال الدین' تھانسیری' شیخ
  عبدالغفور اعظم پوری' شیخ بھورو' شیخ عبدالاحد" (p. 228, folio 224): the book's usual separator stands
  between "شیخ جلال الدین" and "تھانسیری" as between the other names, **so it cannot be told from the
  page whether that is one khalifa with a nisba or two khalifas**. Note also "شیخ عبدالکبیر" here beside
  "شیخ الکبیر" among the sons two paragraphs above, and "شیخ عبدالاحد" here beside the "شیخ عبدالاحد"
  who appears twice at p. 211 (folio 207) — **the chunk does not say whether these are the same men.**
- **A murid list whose count is uncertain.** Sixteen items as punctuated, but "میر زاہد مداری
  عبدالرحمن" (p. 273, folio 269) may be two names with a missing separator, which would make
  **seventeen**. Three of the names are flagged.
- **Which shaykh received the ثواب at Herat is not stated** (p. 225, folio 221): the Lamaat episode names
  "شیخ فخرالدین عراقی" as the book's author and "شیخ صدرالدین تونوی[OCR?]" as the object of praise, then
  says Jami "حضرت شیخ کی روح مبارک کو ثواب پہنچایا". **Neither the dream's content nor the identity of
  "حضرت شیخ" is given**, and "حضرت مولانا" is not explicitly identified with "حضرت جامی[OCR?]".
- **Two spellings of one pir's name across adjacent chapters**: "حضرت خواجہ حسن[OCR?] ناگوری" (p. 217,
  folio 213) and "حضرت خواجہ حسین ناگوری" (p. 220, folio 216). The first is flagged, the second is not.
  **They may be one figure; the chunk does not say so.**
- **Two spellings of one name inside four lines**: "شیخ محمد دہلوی نے جو شیخ الاسلام تھے" and then "آپ نے
  شیخ محمود[OCR?] دہلوی سے کہا" of the same man in the same anecdote (p. 218, folio 214).
- **Two spellings of one name inside one page**: "باوا شیل[OCR?] پوری" and "باوا سمیل[OCR?] پوری"
  (p. 211, folio 207), both flagged; and "حضرت شاہ ہاشم بنوتی[OCR?]" against "ہاشم نجوتوی[OCR?]" in the

- **One name printed two ways in three lines**: "حضرت خواجہ نقش بند" and then "حضرت خواجہ نقش بندیہ"
  (p. 303, folio 299), the second an adjectival form used as a personal name. Neither is flagged.

#### D. Passages that must not be quoted as texts, and glosses that do not carry their originals

- **The pir's Arabic dua** (p. 222, folio 218) reads "اللھم ارجع الجمالی الینا سالمًا" — *return Jamali
  **to us*** — while the printed ترجمہ has "پہنچا دے جمالی کو میرے پاس", *to me*. It also carries four
  flags — "ما نمادر[OCR?]", "زقنا[OCR?]", "مشابدۃ[OCR?]", "نور ینی[OCR?]" — and **should not be quoted
  as a text.**
- **The bisharat** is printed "ترا ولایت بالا تر[OCR?] عطا کی" and glossed "(تجھ کو میں نے ولایت بالادست
  عطا کی)": *بالادست* in the gloss where the quoted words have *بالا تر[OCR?]* (p. 228, folio 224).
- **Jami's two questions** are printed "از نخنان[OCR?] جمالی چیزے وای"[OCR?] and "طبع شعرواری"[OCR?] and
  glossed "(کیا جمالی کی کوئی چیز یاد ہے۔)" and "(تم شعر کہتے ہو)"; the Persian as printed does not carry
  those readings, and the same holds for "میان خرو تو چو فرق است"[OCR?] glossed "(گدھے میں اور تجھ میں
  کیا فرق ہے)" (p. 223, folio 219). **Quote the phrase as printed and the gloss as printed; do not
  harmonise.**
- **Sikandar Lodhi's verse letter should not be quoted as a text.** Fourteen readings carry flags —
  "سالک[OCR?]", "بسے[OCR?]", "بالخیر[OCR?]", "درو[OCR?]", "جابرس[OCR?]", "بکشائے[OCR?]",
  "بسوائے[OCR?]", "کم گام[OCR?]", "توطیاں[OCR?]", "درفغان زاست[OCR?]", "خضر مائی[OCR?]",
  "مایبائی[OCR?]", "دو دیدہ رانور[OCR?]", "مہ نشو[OCR?]" — and the hemistich "بکشائے[OCR?] بسوائے[OCR?]
  در کم گام[OCR?]" stands without its pair at the foot of p. 223 (folio 219) (pp. 223–224,
  folios 219–220).
- **A Persian original at odds with the book's own printed gloss, unflagged.** "ماشنیدہ ایم کہ پیر شما
  آمادہ است و برقص و سماع مشغول است" glossed "ہم نے سنا ہے کہ تمہارے پیر آئے ہیں اور وہ رقص و سماع میں
  مشغول ہیں" (pp. 241–242, folios 237–238): **the Persian says "آمادہ است" (is ready); the gloss says
  "آئے ہیں" (has come).** The same passage prints "مای خواہم" and "دیگر آں رامی رقصد", neither flagged,
  where the gloss reads them as "ہم چاہتے ہیں" and "دوسروں کو بھی". **Quote each as printed.**
- **A Persian passage printed with no gloss at all** — the hinge of باب 51: "عالمیاں امروز برائے کسب
  سلوک طریقت و حصول دولت بر درباب[OCR?] شیخ داؤدی روندہ ایشاں آں چناں نعمت درون خانہ گزاشتہ بیرون می
  روند" (p. 256, folio 252). The book renders **none** of it into Urdu; one word is flagged. **Quote it
  only as printed; do not supply a translation.**
- **An Arabic original withheld and only its Urdu given.** "عربی میں فرمایا، جس کا ترجمہ حسب ذیل ہے",
  followed only by "اے ابو المعالی! تیرا شیخ میرا دل ہے اور دل بجانب چپ ہی ہوتا ہے" (p. 257,
  folio 253). **The Arabic is not in the chunk**, and the word "چپ" carries its Persian sense (left)
  where an Urdu reader would read "silent" — **the pun is in the print, not in the transcription. Cite
  the Urdu as the book's own translation, not as a quotation of the original.**
- **Two Urdu glosses that do not carry all of their Persian.** "منتظر ساعد[OCR?] و وقت باش" is glossed
  only "اس گھڑی کا انتظار کرو" — one of the two nouns is dropped, and it is the flagged one (p. 256,

  تمہارے ہال[OCR?] سے خبردار ہیں" — the Persian runs "مادر" as one word where the gloss reads it as two,
  and the gloss's key word is flagged (p. 259, folio 255).
- **Abul Muali's ghazal should not be quoted as a text**: flags on "خضرتش[OCR?]" and "سروقد[OCR?]"
  (p. 258, folio 254). **Two of his three pen-names are flagged** — "غربی"[OCR?] and "مسلے"[OCR?]
  (pp. 257–258, folios 253–254).

  طالب کتے)", the connective flagged (p. 284, folio 280).

#### E. Footnote, citation and numbering faults

- **باب 42's footnote block is one item short**: three حواشی are listed (p. 219, folio 215) but the text
  carries a marker "4" at the "فرمان" (p. 219, folio 215); **that quotation's source is not in the
  chunk.**
- **باب 43 prints one حاشیہ line for two markers**: "حاشیہ: 1-2- اخبار الاخیار (اردو ترجمہ) ص 546"
  (p. 220, folio 216), **so the death year "940ھ" and the quoted saying rest on the same single
  citation.**
- **باب 41's حاشیہ 1 cites "اخبار الاخیار (اردو ترجمہ) صفحہ 407" and its حاشیہ 2 "صفحہ 48"** (p. 216,
  folio 212) — the death year "961ھ" rests on p. 48 of the same work whose p. 407 supplied the
  parentage.
- **باب 44's حواشی spell one source two ways**, "دربار اکبری" (4) and "درباری اکبری" (8) (p. 226,
  folio 222). A footnote marker in the closing entry of chunk 011 is itself flagged, "4[OCR?]" (p. 211,
  folio 207), and that entry's حواشی block lists five items (p. 213, folio 209).
- **A footnote numbering fault in باب 48.** The حواشی block lists **five** items 1–5 (p. 240, folio 236),
  but the text carries marker **"3" twice** — at the end of "سلاطین سے تعلقات" and again at the end of
  "شادی و اولاد" (p. 238, folio 234) — and **marker 5 never appears**. حواشی 3 and 5 are both
  "تاریخ فرشتہ (جلد دوم' فارسی) صفحہ 403", so the source is the same either way, but **do not cite حاشیہ
  5 for any specific statement** without re-reading the page. Neither marker is flagged.
- **حاشیہ 10 of the orphan block is printed "اخبار اخیار"** without the alif-lam that every other
  citation in that chunk carries; **it is not OCR-flagged** (p. 231, folio 227).
- **Footnote numerals with possible digit slips, all flagged**: حاشیہ 1 of باب 52 cites "جواہر مجددیہ۔
  صفحہ 11٠10[OCR?]" and حاشیہ 3 "معارف لدنیہ (اردو ترجمہ) صفحہ 31٠30[OCR?]"; حاشیہ 4 cites "مبدو
  معار[OCR?] (اردو ترجمہ) صفحہ 11" where the book's own title-list gives the work as "مبدو معاد"
  (pp. 266, 268; folios 262, 264). **حاشیہ 5 is the bare "مکتوب 197", with no collection named** (p. 268,
  folio 264).
- **Markers without the ؎ sign, and a marker used twice.** In the unnamed Lahore entry, markers 5 to 15
  appear as **bare digits** (pp. 270–275, folios 266–271) while the کرامات marker is printed "15؎"
  (p. 276, folio 272) — **15 is used twice**. The حواشی block lists items 1 to 15 (p. 277, folio 273)
  but **markers 1 to 4 have no anchors inside the chunk**, and حاشیہ 4 ("سکینۃ اولیاء ص 19") is out of
  page order relative to حواشی 1–3 (ص 20, 20, 101).
- **باب 55's first marker is printed "11؎ [OCR?]"** where the sequence expects "1؎", the next being "2؎"
  on the same page (p. 281, folio 277). **Treat the father's burial at Delhi as the book's statement,
  not as sourced, until the page is re-read.**
- **A حاشیہ with no anchor.** The Agra entry's block lists **thirteen** items (p. 291, folio 287) but the
  text of pp. 286–291 carries markers only for **8, 9, 10, 11 and 12**; **حاشیہ 13 ("اسرار ابو العلی
  ص 70") has no anchor anywhere** — the jogi-and-Jumna-sand karamat shows no marker. **Do not attribute

  (p. 299, folio 295), where (1), (2) and (3) carry no such mark.
