### pp. 304–392 (chunks 016–020)

#### A. Cross-chunk joins made in this consolidation — every one is an inference, not a printed statement

**Four entries in this range are cut by a chunk seam, and in four cases the two halves can be joined
from the chunk notes alone. None of these joins is a sentence the book prints in one place. Each is
recorded here for a human to confirm against the page images before any fact crosses from one half to
the other.**

1. **باب 63 = pp. 340–343 (folios 336–339): the Kasur entry.** Chunk 017 has the heading "باب 63 /
   حضرت بلھے شاہ رحمۃ اللہ علیہ", the name "عبداللہ شاہ" and the kunya "بلھے شاہ" (p. 340, folio 336)
   but **no mazar and no death year**. Chunk 018 has "آپ کا وصال 1171ھ میں ہوا۔ مزار قصور میں واقع ہے"
   and the takhallus (p. 343, folio 339) but **no heading and no personal name**. The pages are
   consecutive and the next chapter heading is باب 64 at p. 347 (folio 343). **The join makes the
   `mazar-of-bulleh-shah` mapping secure; without it each half is short of a mapping test.**
2. **باب 61 = pp. 322–331 (folios 318–327): the Kahram entry.** Chunk 016's باب 61,
   **حضرت میران سید شاہ بھیکہ**, breaks off at p. 324 (folio 320) at "آپ نے چھ مہینے میں", inside a
   passage in which **شاہ جلال** prescribes "قرآن مجید"، "گلستاں" and "بوستان" and predicts he will
   become **خلیفہ مکتب** within days (p. 324, folio 320). Chunk 017's unnamed subject at p. 325
   (folio 321) has finished "کلام اللہ ' گلستان" and "بوستان" and "خلیفہ مکتب کے فرائض بحسن و خوبی
   انجام دیئے". The pages are consecutive; the next heading is باب 62 at p. 332 (folio 328); the
   epithet "میران" / "میران جی" used throughout pp. 325–331 matches the kunya "میران سید شاہ بھیکہ"
   (p. 322, folio 318); the family's move to **کہرام** in chunk 016 (p. 323, folio 319) matches the
   mazar at **کہرام** in chunk 017 (p. 329, folio 325); and the apparatus dovetails exactly — chunk 016
   records that باب 61's markers **1–6** appear in the text and its حواشی fall outside that chunk, while
   chunk 017 records a **thirteen**-item حواشی block at p. 331 (folio 327) of which only markers **7–13**
   are anchored in its own pages. **Neither chunk worker made this join. If it holds, the death
   "5 رمضان المبارک 1131ھ" and the mazar at کہرام belong to Miran Sayyid Shah Bhikka, and so does the
   offering-sharing rule and the open langar at p. 329 (folio 325). No archive row is created by it —
   Kahram has none — but the material stops being anonymous.**
3. **باب 66 = pp. 354–361 (folios 350–357): the Bareilly entry.** Chunk 018's باب 66,
   **حضرت شاہ نیاز احمد**, breaks off inside a numbered اذکار و اشغال list at "(38) شغل جامع" (p. 359,
   folio 355). Chunk 019's p. 360 (folio 356) opens with a numbered series running **(39) to (58)**.
   Chunk 018 gives the mazar at **بریلی** and the eldest son **حضرت شاہ نظام حسین / شاہ نظام الدین
   حسین** (pp. 356–357, folios 352–353); chunk 019 gives the fire at **بریلی** and **محلہ قطب** and the
   elder son **حضرت شاہ نظام الدین حسین** (pp. 360–361, folios 356–357), and the name reaches the reader
   only inside the karamat, as "وہ نیاز احمد میں ہی ہوں" and "(حضرت شاہ نیاز رحمۃ اللہ علیہ کی)"
   (p. 361, folio 357). **The list's numbering is the decisive element. Not an archive row either way.**
4. **باب 69 = pp. 378–381 (folios 374–377): the Ganj Muradabad entry.** Chunk 019's باب 69,
   **حضرت مولانا شاہ فضل رحمٰن**, breaks off after the "وصیت" heading (p. 379, folio 375), having stated
   that his second marriage was at **گنج مراد آباد**, where he then settled, and that
   "حضرت احمد میاں اور بی بی شفقت" were born of it (p. 379, folio 375). Chunk 020's unnamed subject dies
   "22 ربیع الاول 1313ھ" with his mazar at **گنج مراد آباد (یو پی)** and his son **حضرت احمد میاں** as
   **سجادہ نشین** (p. 380, folio 376). **Same town, same son. Chunk 020 independently guessed the
   chapter number ("presumably باب 69, since باب 70 begins on the next page") without the content
   evidence; the content evidence now agrees.**
5. **Not joined: the Kalpi saint of pp. 304–305 (folios 300–301).** His heading, name and chapter number
   fall **before p. 304**, i.e. outside this range entirely. **Attribute nothing on pp. 304–305 to any
   named saint until chunk 015 is read.** What is fixed about him: pir **حضرت سید امیر ابو العلی**,
   affiliation **ابو العلائیہ / نقش بندیہ**, death **26 شعبان 1071ھ**, mazar **کالپی**, son and sajjada
   nashin **حضرت سید احمد**, second khalifa **شیخ محمد افضل**.
6. **Two other cross-chunk gains, smaller but worth recording.** (a) **خواجہ نور محمد مہاروی** is given
   **no mazar, no death year and no silsila** in chunk 019 (p. 364, folio 360), where the chunk worker
   called him "a candidate figure for the archive"; chunk 018 gives him a death, "2 ذی الحجہ 1205ھ", and
   a mazar, "مزار فیض آثار مہارو میں واقع ہے" (p. 348, folio 344). (b) **حضرت مولانا فخر الدین فخر
   جہاں** is given no mazar in chunks 017 or 018; chunk 019 places his **mazar at دہلی**, where Khwaja
   Muhammad Sulaiman kept forty days' i'tikaf after his passing (p. 364, folio 360). **Neither figure
   has an archive row.**

#### B. Placement doubts: names that could match an archive row, and rows actively excluded

7. **garh-maharaja-shorkot: three fields disagree, and all three values are recorded unconverted.**
   - **Death year.** Book: "یکم جمادی الثانی 1102ھ" (p. 316, folio 312). Row: `figure_died` **1691**
     (CE). **No conversion is made here**; a human should decide whether these are the same event.
   - **Silsila.** Book: **"قادریہ مسروریہ"**, on the saint's own authority (p. 316, folio 312). Row:
     **"Sarwari Qadiri"**. **The book never uses the word "سروری" anywhere in this range. This is a
     substantive disagreement over the order's name, not an OCR artefact — none of the three words is
     flagged.**
   - **Place-name.** The row reads "Garh Maharaja (Shorkot)"; the book names only **شور کوٹ** and,
     twice, **شورکٹ** (pp. 313, 316; folios 309, 312), and **never** "گڑھ مہاراجہ". **Whether the book's
     Shorkot is the row's Garh Maharaja is an identification the book does not make.**
8. **سلطان باہو at p. 349 (folio 345) is not mapped, and this is deliberate.** He appears only as the
   subject of a single anecdote told by another saint in a debate on "مسئلہ نفی وجود" — **no town, no
   district, no mazar, no death year and no silsila.** Bare name; left open, and **not folded into the
   garh-maharaja-shorkot entry**.
9. **mazar-of-bulleh-shah: the death year conflicts and the silsila is absent.** Book "1171ھ" (p. 343,
   folio 339) against the row's "1757"; **recorded as printed, with no conversion.** The row's figure
   reads "Bulleh Shah (Abdullah Shah Qadri)"; **the book supplies عبداللہ شاہ and بلھے شاہ but never
   "Qadri" for him personally** (p. 340, folio 336) — the nisba in the entry belongs to his pir.
   **Kasur is named first as his place of study (p. 340, folio 336) and only later as his shrine town
   (p. 343, folio 339).**
10. **shrine-of-shah-inayat-qadiri is the weaker of the two Kasur-side mappings and is one statement
    short of secure.** The book gives residence at **لاہور** (p. 341, folio 337), the nisba **قادری**
    (pp. 341–342, folios 337–338) and the role of pir (p. 342, folio 338); it gives **no mazar, no death
    year, no silsila-name beyond "قادری", and no locality within Lahore**. The row reads "Shah Inayat
    Qadiri", "Mozang Chungi, Queens Road, Lahore", "c. 1728", "Qadri Shattari". **The book prints the
    name as "شاہ عنایت اللہ قادری" and "عنایت اللہ قادری" — with اللہ, where the row has none.**
11. **Do not conflate the book's two "Inayat" figures.** "حضرت شاہ ایت اللہ علوی" [so printed, **not**
    OCR-flagged], ancestor of Shah Niaz Ahmad, who left the throne of Bukhara for **ملتان** (p. 354,
    folio 350), is a different man from شاہ عنایت اللہ قادری of Lahore, **and the printed form is one
    syllable short of عنایت اللہ. Record as printed; do not emend.**
12. **darbar-abul-muali-qadri is actively excluded, and the exclusion should be recorded.** The row
    reads "Darbar Abul Muali Qadri", Lahore, "Hazrat Syed Muhammad Khair ul Deen, known as Shah Abul
    Muali Qadri", "16 Rabi ul Awal 1024 AH". The book's **حضرت شاہ ابو المعالی** lives at
    "اینتھہ [OCR?]" (pp. 326–328, folios 322–324) — **never at Lahore** — and is the **living pir** who
    initiates, summons, corrects, invests with khilafat and travels to Kahram to see a disciple who died
    in **1131ھ** (pp. 326–329, folios 322–325). **The differing location alone forbids the map; the
    chronology compounds it. Two men of the same name, or a different Shah Abu al-Maali. Note that his
    town is itself OCR-flagged on every occurrence.**
13. **shrine-of-mauj-darya-bukhari is excluded on two counts and the resemblance is name-only.** The row
    has "Hazrat Meeran Muhammad Shah Bukhari (Mauj Darya)", Lahore, died 1604, Suhrawardi. The book's
    "میران" / "میران جی" has his mazar at **کہرام** and died "5 رمضان المبارک 1131ھ" (p. 329,
    folio 325). **Different town, and a book-year that cannot be reconciled with the row's CE year
    without a conversion this consolidation does not make.**
14. **حضرت خواجہ محمد سلیمان at p. 348 (folio 344) is not mapped**, though باب 67's subject at
    pp. 363–368 (folios 359–364) **is**. The p. 348 occurrence is a bare name inside
    خواجہ نور محمد مہاروی's khalifa list — **no nisba, no town, no date**. The book's own two statements
    agree on name and pir (p. 348, folio 344; p. 364, folio 360), **but joining them is a judgement and
    both readings are left standing.**
15. **حافظ محمد جمال ملتانی is not mapped, though the name matches element for element.** He resembles
    **shrine-of-hafiz-muhammad-jamal-multani** ("Near Aam Khas Bagh, outside Daulat Gate, Multan City,
    Punjab"; "Hafiz Muhammad Jamal Multani"; died 1811; Chishti). The book's "حافظ محمد جمال ملتانی"
    carries the row's nisba and stands in the khalifa list of a saint whose chain the book traces to
    Fakhr al-Din Fakhr Jahan of Delhi (p. 348, folio 344). **But the range gives him no shrine
    statement, no death year and no silsila-name — a nisba inside a list is not a placement. This is the
    likeliest of the bare-name cases to be a genuine match and a human should settle it.**
16. **پاک پٹن is named with no shrine and no saint attached.** "حضرت مولانا کے ساتھ آپ پاک پٹن گئے تھے"
    (p. 348, folio 344) is the whole statement. The archive's **shrine-of-fariduddin-ganjshakar** is at
    Pakpattan; **the range does not connect the visit to it, to any mazar, or to any occasion, so nothing
    is mapped and nothing should be inferred from the trip.**
17. **کوٹ مٹھن is named but the madrasa passage is not mapped.** The book places
    **قاضی محمد عاقل**'s **مدرسہ** at **کوٹ مٹھن** and Khwaja Muhammad Sulaiman's studies there (p. 363,
    folio 359). This matches the location of **mithankot-kot-mithan**, **but the passage says nothing
    about that shrine, nothing about Khwaja Ghulam Farid, and nothing about a mazar, urs or custodian at
    Kot Mithan — only a town and a madrasa.** Under the mapping rule this is not a match.
18. **The one mithankot-kot-mithan mapping in this range rests on a single line of a bookseller's
    catalogue** (p. 392) and **yields no fact about the shrine, the saint or his dates**. **A
    consolidator who would rather not have an archive row "informed" by an advertisement should move
    that subsection to the unmapped list; nothing is lost.**
19. **اچ is named but not mapped, and could not be resolved to one row in any case.** The bai'at takes
    place at **اچ** because Khwaja Nur Muhammad Maharvi came there (p. 364, folio 360). The archive has
    five Uch rows (`shrine-of-jalaluddin-surkh-posh-bukhari-jalaluddin-bukhari`,
    `shrine-of-makhdoom-jahaniyan-jahangasht`, `tomb-of-baha-al-halim-uch-sharif`,
    `tomb-of-javindi-bibi`, `tomb-of-ustad-nuriya`); **the range mentions none of their figures, shrines
    or dates. Leave unmapped; never pick.** Note that **اچ** is also named as the Kasur saint's father's
    home town (p. 340, folio 336), with equally no shrine statement.
20. **مہار / مہارو has no archive row and the range gives it a mazar but no row to attach it to**
    (p. 348, folio 344). **خواجہ نور محمد مہاروی is a candidate figure for the archive, not a match to
    it.**
21. **The name "شاہ نیاز احمد" appears inside an archive row's silsila field, and this is a resemblance
    only.** The **wasif-ali-wasif** row records allegiance to "Hazrat Shah Niyaz Ahmad Chisti Qadri".
    **That row is a Lahore shrine of a different figure** (Wasif Ali Wasif, Miani Sahib graveyard,
    d. 18 January 1993); the range says nothing whatever about Wasif Ali Wasif, and it places this
    saint's mazar and khanqah at **بریلی** in India (p. 356, folio 352; p. 360, folio 356), with
    takhallus "نیاز" (p. 358, folio 354), death "6 جمادی الثانی 1250ھ" (p. 356, folio 352) and a Qadiri
    initiation via Baghdad with **no order-name printed** (p. 356, folio 352). **This is not a mapping**;
    a human may want to check whether the row's pir-name refers to this man.
22. **Two separate "غریب نواز" traps in this range, and both must be kept away from
    `khawaja-feroz-ud-din-gharib-nawaz`** (row: "Miani Sahib Graveyard, Lahore"; "Hazrat Khawaja
    Feroz-ud-Din Gharib Nawaz Chishti Nizami"; died 1981).
    - **"بی بی غریب نواز" is a laqab of Shah Niaz Ahmad's mother and nothing else**: "آپ کی والدہ ماجدہ
      کا نام بی بی لاڈو ہے۔ ان کو بی بی غریب نواز کے لقب سے پکارا جاتا ہے" (p. 354, folio 350). **A woman
      of Sirhind and Delhi, not connected here to Ajmer or to Lahore.**
    - **"غریب نواز" at اجمیر شریف** (p. 383, folio 379) is named only as the darbar Waris Ali Shah
      visited. **The book gives that figure no personal name, no date and no silsila, so nothing there is
      mappable to any row.**
23. **حاجی وارث علی شاہ is NOT `mausoleum-of-waris-shah`, and the range confirms it with positive
    evidence rather than mere caution.** Book: mazar at **دیوا**, district **بارہ بنکی (یو پی)**
    (pp. 382–383, folios 378–379); death "30 محرم الحرام 1323ھ مطابق 12 اپریل 1905ء" (p. 383,
    folio 379); silsila **وارثی**, founded by him (p. 382, folio 378). Row: "Jandiala Sher Khan,
    Sheikhupura District, Punjab, Pakistan", figure "Waris Shah", died 1798, Chishti. **Shrine town,
    district, country, death year and silsila all differ. Not a candidate.**
24. **Four advertisement names matching archive rows are deliberately left unmapped** (p. 392):
    "حضرت سلطان باہو" (cf. `garh-maharaja-shorkot`), "حضرت بلھے شاہ" (cf. `mazar-of-bulleh-shah`),
    "حضرت بابا فرید الدین مسعود گنج شکر" (cf. `shrine-of-fariduddin-ganjshakar`) and "خواجہ شاہ حسین"
    (cf. `madho-lal-hussain`). **Each is a bare name in a bookseller's list, with no place, no date, no
    pir and no silsila, and a bare name is not a match.**
25. **"شاہ نظام الدین" appears in the Kahram saint's khalifa list, and باب 62's subject bears the same
    name.** The khalifa "شاہ نظام الدین" (p. 329, folio 325) and "حضرت شاہ نظام الدین اورنگ آبادی"
    (p. 332, folio 328) are named in adjacent entries and **the book makes no connection between them**;
    باب 62 gives its subject a different pir entirely (Shaikh Kalimullah at Delhi, p. 334, folio 330).
    **Do not join them.**

#### C. Internal contradictions, arithmetic and headings that fight their content

26. **The count of Sultan Bahu's sons does not close.** The text says "آٹھ لڑکے پیدا ہوئے", **eight
    sons**, names the youngest **سلطان حیات محمد** as having died in childhood, and then lists **eight
    further names** as "آپ کے دیگر صاحب زادوں کے نام" — **nine in all** (p. 316, folio 312). One list
    item is the bare "محمد[OCR?]", which may be a broken fragment of the preceding "سلطان لطیف محمد" or
    a separate son whose first element is lost. **Do not state a number of sons; do not state this list
    as complete.**
27. **باب 62's dates do not close, and both of the disagreeing figures are flagged.** Birth "1060ھ"
    (p. 332, folio 328); death "12 ذیقعدہ 1242ھ [OCR?]" and age "بیاسی [OCR?] سال" (p. 335, folio 331).
    **Birth plus eighty-two lunar years does not reach 1242; the stated age and the stated death year
    cannot both stand with the stated birth year. The death year is the more load-bearing figure and it
    is the one carrying the flag. No year should be taken from this entry without re-reading the page.**
28. **The count of dhikr methods contradicts itself and neither figure is flagged.** "ذکر نفی و اثبات کے
    باون طریقے آپ کو بتائے" — **fifty-two** (p. 356, folio 352) — against the list heading
    "ذکر نفی اثبات 51 طریقے سے" — **fifty-one** (p. 359, folio 355).
29. **The zikr quota carries two numbers that do not agree, and neither is flagged.** "ایک لاکھ مرتبہ"
    and then a penalty turning on "چالیس مرتبہ ہر روز" (p. 330, folio 326). **As printed, the sentence
    does not say whether the lakh is a lifetime total, a completion target, or a figure from which the
    forty is a minimum. Quote the sentence whole; do not reconcile.**
30. **The heir's name contradicts itself across two lines, with a third occurrence in a later chunk.**
    "حضرت شاہ نظام حسین" is named sajjada nashin (p. 356, folio 352); "حضرت شاہ نظام الدین حسین" heads
    the khalifa list on the next page (p. 357, folio 353); and "حضرت شاہ نظام الدین حسین" appears again
    as his elder son (p. 361, folio 357). **Two occurrences of the longer form against one of the
    shorter. Recorded as a tally only; do not reconcile.**
31. **The book's own two Hijri/CE pairings for Haji Waris Ali Shah do not agree in interval.**
    "9 محرم الحرام 1323ھ مطابق 26 مارچ 1905ء" and "30 محرم الحرام 1323ھ مطابق 12 اپریل 1905ء" (p. 383,
    folio 379) are **21 lunar days apart in the Hijri reckoning but 17 days apart in the CE.** **Neither
    date is OCR-flagged. Both are recorded as printed and neither should be converted; a human must
    decide which member of which pair is wrong before either enters an entry.** Note also that the day
    of the illness (9 Muharram) is the eve of Ashura and the death day is given as **30 Muharram**; and
    that he is said to have announced his death a day in advance — "ہم کل صبح چار بجے چلیں گے" (p. 383,
    folio 379) — **which the book's own Hijri reckoning places seven days before the death.**
32. **The travel refusals contradict the travels.** He is said not to sit in a **ریل** or a **جہاز**
    (p. 384, folio 380), yet the same chapter takes him by **جہاز** from Bombay to Jeddah (p. 383,
    folio 379) and hungry aboard ship for days (p. 386, folio 382). **The book does not mark the
    tension; both statements stand.**
33. **A heading whose content contradicts it, in باب 61.** Under "والدہ ماجدہ" (the mother) the text
    reads "آپ کے والد کا نام بی بی ملکو ہے" — **"your father's name is Bibi Malko"** (p. 322,
    folio 318, حاشیہ 3). **The heading and the name both require *mother*; the word printed is والد.
    Quote as printed; carry بی بی ملکو as the mother's name only with this note attached.**
34. **A heading whose content does not match it, in باب 67.** Under "شادی اور اولاد" the text says only
    "آپ کے دونوں صاحب زادے' خواجہ گل محمد اور خواجہ درویش محمد آپ کی حیات میں وفات پا گئے" (p. 365,
    folio 361) — **no marriage and no wife is mentioned at all**, and the two sons appear only as
    predeceased. **Do not infer a marriage or a wife's name from this heading.**
35. **A heading that reverses its own direction of travel.** "کالپی سے[OCR?] آمد" — "arrival **from**
    Kalpi" — heads a passage describing arrival **at** Kalpi: "آپ کالپی تشریف لائے اور سند ہدایت و ارشاد
    پر متمکن ہوئے" (p. 304, folio 300). **The preposition is itself OCR-flagged.**
36. **A doubled sentence in the ازواج و اولاد paragraph.** "لڑکی کا انتقال کے بعد آپ نے دوسری شادی کی۔
    دوسری بیوی سے ایک لڑکی اور دو لڑکے پیدا ہوئے۔" is printed twice in succession at p. 356 (folio 352),
    the second time running straight into "لڑکی کا انتقال صغر سنی میں ہو گیا". **Read as one second
    marriage with one daughter and two sons; the duplication is a transcription or print artefact and
    the page should be re-checked before the family is described.**
37. **A grandfather named two ways, neither flagged.** The mother is "صاحب زادی سید مولانا سعد الدین کی"
    (p. 354, folio 350), while the man who placed his hand on the boy's is "سعید الدین رضوی" and
    "مولانا سعید الدین رضوی" (p. 355, folio 351). **سعد الدین and سعید الدین both stand as printed.**
38. **Sultan Bahu's murshid carries two honorifics on one page.** "حضرت شیخ عبدالرحمان قادری" in Shah
    Habibullah's instruction and "حضرت سید عبدالرحمان قادری" twice in the narrative that follows
    (p. 315, folio 311). **Neither is OCR-flagged. Shaikh or Sayyid is unresolved in the print.**
39. **A pir's name printed short once.** "حضرت امیر العلی رحمۃ اللہ علیہ کی خانقاہ" (p. 306, folio 302),
    where the same page and the pages either side read "حضرت سید امیر ابو العلی". **Not flagged;
    recorded as printed.**
40. **The pir's nisba in باب 62 is spelled four different ways inside one entry, and the fifth form is
    dangerous.** "حضرت شیخ شاہ جہاں آبادی [OCR?]" (p. 332, folio 328); "حضرت شیخ کلیم اللہ شاہجہانی
    آبادی" (p. 332, folio 328); "حضرت شیخ کلیم اللہ آبادی" (p. 333, folio 329); "حضرت شیخ کلیم اللہ
    شاہ جہاں آبادی" (p. 334, folio 330); and, in the variant account, "قطب حضرت شاہ جہانیاں [OCR?]
    آبادی" (p. 334, folio 330). **Read literally the last names a different person, and the form
    "جہانیاں" is precisely the element this book elsewhere attaches to Makhdum Jahaniyan Jahangasht.
    Treat it as a corrupt spelling of the pir's own nisba, flagged, and do not create a figure from it.**
41. **Two spellings of one home town, neither flagged.** The father settled at **"مہار"** (p. 347,
    folio 343); the saint's own وطن and his mazar are at **"مہارو"** (p. 348, folio 344, twice); his
    nisba throughout is "مہاروی".
42. **"بانسبت" as printed** in "صاحب حال اور صاحب مقام ہونا آسان ہے' بانسبت ہونا مشکل ہے" (p. 380,
    folio 376). **Not OCR-flagged**, but the next line reads "صاحب نسبت", and the sense needs
    "صاحب نسبت" here. **Quote as printed; do not harmonise.**
43. **A preposition printed as a different word, unflagged.** "اچ سے سکونت ترک کر کے ملکوال ہیں تشریف
    لائے" (p. 340, folio 336): **"ہیں" stands where "میں" is required and is not flagged.** It does not
    affect the sense but marks the line as a print or transcription slip.
44. **"او" printed where "اور" is expected, unflagged**: "اس ولایتی کا پیٹ اور جسم پھولنے لگا او اس مریض
    کو افاقہ ہونا شروع ہوا" (p. 360, folio 356). Recorded as printed.
45. **The Egypt sentence's syntax does not carry its own sense.** "عربی پاشا کے خدیو مصر کو معزول کرنے
    پر اور خدیو مصر کی انگریزوں سے امداد کرنے پر' برطانوی حکومت نے ہندوستانی فوجیوں کو مصر جانے کا حکم
    دیا" (p. 386, folio 382) — as printed, Arabi Pasha deposes the Khedive **and** the Khedive assists
    the English, and the British order troops to Egypt on both counts. **No `[OCR?]` flag. Record the
    episode as the book's, and do not reconstruct the politics.**
46. **A speech printed only as a parenthetical gloss, with no original.** Shaikh Kalimullah's answer
    appears as "(یہ شخص عزیز (آشنا) ہے' غیر نہیں ہے)" — in the parenthesis-and-gloss format the book
    uses for **translations** of Persian, but **with no Persian above it** (p. 333, folio 329). **The
    original line has evidently dropped out. Quote it as the book's Urdu, not as the shaykh's words.**
47. **The saint's counter-reading of the Quranic verse is a word-order-literal calque with
    interpolations, and one phrase is ungrammatical as printed.** "مس نہیں کرے (قرآن و ادراک معنی و
    اسرار کے کو) مگر پاک لوگ (کہ پاک ہوں آلائش بشری سے)" (p. 330, folio 326) — "قرآن و ادراک معنی و
    اسرار کے کو" doubles the object marker and is **not** flagged. **The substance (moral against ritual
    purity) is clear; the wording should not be quoted as a translation of the verse.**
48. **Internal arithmetic, for the record.** Sayyid Dost Muhammad: birth "996ھ" (p. 306, folio 302),
    death "26 جمادی الثانی 1090ھ", age stated "چورانوے سال" (pp. 307–308, folios 303–304) — ninety-four
    lunar years, **which agrees with the stated years.** Sultan Bahu: birth "1039ھ", death
    "یکم جمادی الثانی 1102ھ" — **sixty-three lunar years apart; the book states no age and none should
    be supplied.** Ghaus Ali Shah: birth "1219ھ", death "26 ربیع الاول 1297ھ" — seventy-eight lunar
    years apart, against a printed age "اٹھتہر[OCR?] سال", **so the agreement is between a flagged word
    and a computation, not between two printed figures.** Khwaja Muhammad Sulaiman: birth "1184ھ", death
    "7 صفر 1267ھ" — eighty-three lunar years apart; **the book states no age.** His internal ages —
    father's death at four, bai'at at sixteen, khilafat at twenty-two (pp. 363–364, folios 359–360) —
    are relative and cannot be checked against the years.
49. **The mazar-and-mosque sentence mixes tenses and cannot be used for a tomb's date** (p. 327,
    folio 323) — see Cross-cutting material. **It establishes proximity at the time of writing, nothing
    about the order of building.**
50. **The sajjada nashini of باب 62 is stated in the present tense** — "سجادہ نشین ہیں", "are", not
    "were" (p. 335, folio 331). **Whether this reflects the compiler's source or the compiler's own
    present is not determinable from the page; do not read it as a statement about the shrine's
    custodianship at the time of printing.**
51. **What became of Friday's offerings is not stated** (p. 336, folio 332) — retained, applied to the
    khanqah, or something else. **(not stated.)**
52. **Whose ilham, and from whom.** The mosque warning is "الہام و مکاشفہ حضرت غوث الاعظم کی طرف اس بات
    کا ہوا" (p. 352, folio 348) — **the direction of the phrase is ambiguous as printed and is not
    OCR-flagged**; the sense taken above is that the warning came from Ghaus al-Azam.
53. **The khalifa list of the Kahram saint yields ten or eleven names depending on its punctuation.**
    "میاں افضل شاہ لطف اللہ جالندھری" (p. 329, folio 325) carries no separator where the rest of the
    list uses "'", and the list is broken once by "۔" after "شیخ نعمت اللہ". **The stated total is
    "بیالیس" (forty-two), so neither reading conflicts with it, but the number of *famous* khalifas
    cannot be fixed from the page.**
54. **An author-name discrepancy inside one advertisement line**: the title is printed "کلام شاہ حسین
    رحمۃ اللہ علیہ" but the author "خواجہ شاہ حسین" — **the honorific "خواجہ" appears only in the author
    slot** (p. 392). Neither is OCR-flagged.
55. **One catalogue "author" slot is a description, not a person**: "ہشت بہشت | مجموعہ خواجگان چشت اہل
    بہشت [OCR?]" (p. 392). **Do not enter "اہل بہشت [OCR?]" as an author.**
56. **Arabic printed without diacritics or with a variant letter, none of it OCR-flagged**: "اقرب الیہ
    من جبل الورید" (p. 384, folio 380); the durood form "الھم صلی اللہ علی محمد وعلی ال محمد وبارک
    وسلم" (p. 385, folio 381); "انی کنت من الظلمین" and "وما خلقت الجن والانس الا لیعبدون" (p. 373,
    folio 369; p. 366, folio 362). **Record exactly as printed; a human with the page images should
    check the letter-forms before any is quoted as scripture.**
57. **Three Persian passages are left unglossed where the same entry glosses its other Persian**: the
    couplet "سپردم بتو مایہ خویش را / تودانی حساب کم و بیش را" (p. 334, folio 330); "حضور! ہنگام
    دستگیری وقت عنایت ست" (p. 338, folio 334); and both signet inscriptions (p. 336, folio 332).
    **Nothing in this consolidation translates them.** The Persian couplets at pp. 373–374 and 377
    (folios 369–370, 373) and the Arabic of the وظائف (p. 373, folio 369) are likewise printed without
    Urdu glosses.

#### D. Apparatus problems: footnote markers, blocks and source titles

58. **An empty حاشیہ under a printed marker, over the largest claim in باب 60.** The حواشی block prints
    "حاشیہ: 4-" with **no source after it** (p. 321, folio 317), while marker 4 in the text is attached
    to the vision of the Prophet, the bai'at at his hand and the handing over to Abd al-Qadir Jilani
    (p. 314, folio 310). **That episode is therefore unsourced in the print. Attribute it to this book,
    not to مناقب سلطانی.**
59. **Two karamat in باب 67 with no footnote anchors at all.** The merchant story and the revived-boy
    story (pp. 366–368, folios 362–364) carry **no markers**, while the entry's six حواشی are exhausted
    by the lineage, bai'at, books, death date and sama' statements. **Attribute both stories to the book
    itself, not to انوار العارفین, until the pages are re-read.**
60. **باب 70's کرامات, تعلیمات and whole biography carry no footnote markers** (pp. 382–387,
    folios 378–383), while the اقوال carry 1–20 and the اوراد و وظائف 21–24. **Attribute the ship
    miracle, the Egypt episode and the whole biography to the book, not to حیات وارث**, until a human
    checks the page images for markers the transcription may have dropped.
61. **باب 66's حواشی block falls outside this range's chunk boundaries as transcribed.** Markers 1 to 9
    appear in the text (pp. 355–359, folios 351–355) with **no حواشی block transcribed**, because the
    chapter breaks across the chunk seam; chunk 019 records the block for the same entry's second half
    as twelve items, all "کرامات نظامیہ", at p. 362 (folio 358), of which **only markers 10, 11 and 12**
    are anchored in chunk 019's pages. **Markers 1–9 anchor to pp. 355–359. Do not attach حواشی 1–9 to
    anything recorded from pp. 360–361, and note that the two halves of this block were seen by
    different workers.**
62. **باب 61's حواشی block is thirteen items long and only seven are anchored in chunk 017's pages.**
    The block on p. 331 (folio 327) lists thirteen; the markers transcribed in chunk 017 are 7 (p. 325),
    8 (p. 325), 9 (p. 327), 10 (p. 328), 11 (p. 328), 12 (p. 329) and 13 (p. 330). **Items 1–6 belong to
    pp. 322–324, i.e. to chunk 016's pages, where chunk 016 independently records that markers 1–6
    appear in the text.** See join 2 above.
63. **باب 69's حواشی block falls outside the chunk that carries its markers.** Markers **1** (after the
    hadith sanad) and **2** (after the second marriage) appear at p. 379 (folio 375); the block is not
    transcribed there. Chunk 020's Ganj Muradabad entry has a five-item block, all
    "مسالک السالکین جلد دوم", at p. 381 (folio 377). **If join 4 holds, these belong to one entry and a
    human should check whether the five-item block covers markers 1–5 of the whole of باب 69.**
64. **A stray numeral where a marker would sit, and it cannot be one.** "آپ فرماتے ہیں کہ 8 [OCR?]"
    (p. 380, folio 376) puts a flagged "8" where a marker would go, but that entry's حواشی run **only to
    5** (p. 381, folio 377). **The "8" cannot be its marker and may be a stray or a misread word.**
65. **باب 62's block is complete and fully anchored** — twelve items on p. 339 (folio 335), markers 1–12
    all present in the text of pp. 332–336 (folios 328–332). **The one clean apparatus in the range.**
66. **A source title flagged and pageless, carrying a whole text on its own.** باب 59's حاشیہ 3 reads
    "نجات قاسم[OCR?]" with no page number (p. 309, folio 305). **It is the only authority given for the
    پیم کہانی; the title should not be relied on as read.**
67. **A footnote with no page number where every other in its block has one.** باب 64's حاشیہ 4 reads
    "مناقب المحجوبین [OCR?]" alone (p. 350, folio 346), **supporting the saint's personal name
    "سہیل"**; حواشی 1–3 and 5–11 all carry a page. **The name "سہیل" therefore rests on an incompletely
    cited source.** Similarly باب 67's حاشیہ 6, "مناقب محجوبین[OCR?]", has **no page reference** where
    the other five in its block do (p. 368, folio 364).
68. **A footnote page number out of sequence and OCR-flagged.** In باب 61's block, حاشیہ 8 reads
    "انوار العارفین (فارسی) ص 45 [OCR?]" where its neighbours run 413, 413, 413, 414, 414, 415, 416,
    417, 417 and "414٬420" (p. 331, folio 327). **"ص 45" is very likely a lost digit but is recorded as
    printed.** حاشیہ 12's "ص 414٬420" prints two page numbers separated by a mark.
69. **A page number lost inside a heading.** "دعاء سیفی : آپ فرماتے ہیں۔15 ص[illegible]" (p. 320,
    folio 316) — a stray "ص" plus an illegible number sits in the body text where a page citation
    appears to have been set.
70. **Possible digit slips in باب 65's حواشی, as printed.** حواشی 2، 4، 5 cite "انوار العارفین (فارسی)
    ص 522" while حاشیہ 7 cites the same work at "ص 23" and حاشیہ 8 at "ص 22" (p. 353, folio 349);
    حاشیہ 3 reads "کرامات نظامیہ ص 19،20[OCR?]". **Only the last is OCR-flagged; the others are recorded
    as printed.**
71. **A suspect page pair in باب 68.** حاشیہ 13 reads "سیرت غوثیہ ص 66،27[OCR?]" — the second figure is
    OCR-flagged and **runs backwards** against حاشیہ 12 (ص 64،65) and حاشیہ 14 (ص 66) (p. 375,
    folio 371).
72. **Footnote pairs that repeat their page numbers, none of them OCR-flagged.** In the Ganj Muradabad
    entry, حواشی 1 and 2 are both "مسالک السالکین جلد دوم ص 180،181" and حواشی 4 and 5 both "ص 191،193"
    (p. 381, folio 377). In باب 70, حواشی 1 and 2 are both "حیات وارث ص 224،255" and حواشی 3 and 4 both
    "ص 269" (pp. 387–388, folios 383–384). **"ص 224،255" is not a consecutive pair where the book's other
    paired citations are, so "255" may be a misprint or misreading for "225" — recorded as printed, not
    corrected.**
73. **A source-title variant across the book.** This range prints **"مسالک السالکین"** (p. 381,
    folio 377, all five حواشی); **the notes for chunk 004 of the same book record the same work as
    "سالک السالکین"**. **Neither form is OCR-flagged in its chunk. A human should decide which is the
    print's form before either is cited; the two may be a genuine inconsistency in the book.** The same
    caution applies to **"مناقب المحجوبین [OCR?]"** (p. 350, folio 346) against **"مناقب
    محجوبین[OCR?]"** (p. 368, folio 364), both flagged.
74. **The back-cover list is a sales catalogue, not the book's bibliography** (p. 392). **The
    compendium's chain of transmission is carried only in the per-chapter حواشی and must be
    reconstructed chunk by chunk.**

#### E. Flagged readings that land on a name, a date or a numeral, carried through by page

**Every flag below is carried onto the exact word it was attached to in the chunk notes.**

- **p. 304 (folio 300)**: "کالپی سے[OCR?]" — **the heading's preposition, which reverses the direction
  of travel**.
- **p. 306 (folio 302)**: "معہ مریدین و معتقدین[OCR?]".
- **p. 308 (folio 304)**: in the پیم کہانی, "جھری[OCR?]", "سینور[OCR?]", "سانکری[OCR?]", "نمیں[OCR?]",
  "جیور[OCR?]" — **five flags in ten lines; do not quote the پیم کہانی as a text**.
- **p. 309 (folio 305)**: the source title "نجات قاسم[OCR?]", pageless.
- **p. 313 (folio 309)**: "آپ کے نام میں "ہوا"[OCR?] آتا ہے" — **the whole point of the name-gloss rests
  on that reading, and it is the one word in the sentence that is flagged. Do not state what the name
  باہو is said to contain.**
- **p. 316 (folio 312)**: "محمد[OCR?]" in the son-list; "حضرت نورنگ[OCR?]", "(کھتران)[OCR?]",
  "حضرت ملامعانی[OCR?]" in the khalifa-list — **two of Sultan Bahu's three named khalifas and one of
  their two regional tags are unsafe to quote**.
- **p. 317 (folio 313)**: in the Persian verses, "مقسم[OCR?]", "بہاول[OCR?]", "دز ہوا[OCR?]",
  "دانما[OCR?]", "بالیقیں[OCR?]" — six flagged words in all, plus a final couplet set as two short
  lines where the rest are hemistich pairs. **Do not quote the verses as a text.**
- **p. 318 (folio 314)**: "ذکر خفی اس طرح سے پچانا[OCR?] جاتا ہے… اس کا ایک ذرہ مشرف[OCR?] تک کل مخلوق
  کو طے[OCR?] تو وہ ایسا بے ہوش اور مست ہو جائے" — **three flagged words leave the conditional clause
  without sense. Do not paraphrase this sentence.**
- **p. 319 (folio 315)**: "اگر اس کا ایک فعل بھی شرع محمدی کے خلافت[OCR?] ہے تو وہ صوفی نہیں' بلکہ
  شیطان ہے" — **the flagged "خلافت" stands where "خلاف" (contrary to) is required. Carry the flag.**
- **p. 320 (folio 316)**: "سورہ مزل[OCR?]" at the heading, **unflagged four times in the passages that
  follow — do not resolve the sura's name; quote as printed**; and "ص[illegible]" inside the
  دعاء سیفی heading.
- **p. 322 (folio 318)**: "واپس[OCR?]" standing where a "hence/therefore" belongs in the entry's opening
  nasab sentence; "حسب اشارت پر بشارت[OCR?]".
- **p. 323 (folio 319)**: "خاندانی جھگڑوں اور اہل خاندان کے حد[OCR?] کے باعث" — **family quarrels are
  stated; the second cause of the move from Sewana is unreadable**; "اس کے جڑے[OCR?] ٹوٹ گئے".
- **p. 325 (folio 321)**: "ملوی[OCR?]", "پدرہ[OCR?] کوس" — **the distance from Kahram cannot be
  quoted**.
- **p. 326 (folio 322)**: "آپ [illegible] شاہ بھاول کا مشورہ قبول کیا", "آپ سے [OCR?] حضرت شاہ بھاول",
  "اینتھہ [OCR?]", "ملوی [OCR?]" — **which of the two men set out, and from whom, is lost in the flagged
  stretch**.
- **p. 327 (folio 323)**: "کہرام [OCR?]", "آفتاب غروب [OCR?]".
- **p. 328 (folio 324)**: "بصد [OCR?] عاجزی".
- **p. 329 (folio 325)**: "حضرت نظام الدین اولیاء رحمۃ اللہ علیہ [OCR?]" **twice, the flag falling on
  the honorific rather than the name**.
- **p. 330 (folio 326)**: "موضع نوندھن [OCR?]" — **the village of the boy's resurrection cannot be

#### F. Passages that should not be quoted as texts

- The eight Persian lines in praise of Abd al-Qadir Jilani (p. 317, folio 313) — six flags.
- The پیم کہانی (p. 308, folio 304) — five flags in ten lines.
- The ذکر خفی passage at its climax (p. 318, folio 314) — three flags.
- The Persian and Urdu verses at p. 358 (folio 354) — five flags.
- The numbered اذکار و اشغال list (pp. 359–360, folios 355–356) — seven flags in the first thirty-eight
  items, fourteen in the eighteen "دیگر اشغال".
- The mazar-door chronogram (p. 352, folio 348) — three flags; **and the bracketed "(1207 ہجری)" after
  the verses is the book's own resolution of the chronogram, printed as such; the numerical value of the
  words is not computed here.**
- The saint's counter-reading of "لا یمسہ الا المطھرون" (p. 330, folio 326) — ungrammatical as printed.
- The heading "سبع [OCR?] مہر" and the final قول "تاقہ [OCR?] کی قدر جانے" (pp. 336–337,
  folios 332–333).
- The Masnavi question and answer (p. 380, folio 376).

#### G. Provenance of the transcription itself

- **Chunks 016–019 (pp. 304–379) carry no publisher's rights line, price line, digitisation stamp or
  library watermark anywhere.** The transcribed matter is body text, verse, sub-headings, chapter
  headings, حواشی blocks, part dividers and blank pages only.
- **Chunk 020's back matter (pp. 390–392) likewise carries no digitisation stamp, no library watermark,
  no rights line and no price** — only the publisher's endpaper and the sales catalogue, and both are
  transcribed from described page images rather than reproduced.
- **Blank pages, with the folios they consume**: p. 310 (folio 306), p. 312 (folio 308), p. 344
  (folio 340), p. 346 (folio 342), p. 376 (folio 372), p. 389 (folio 385). **All are marked
  `[blank page]` and print no marker.**
- **Two non-agreeing publisher addresses inside this range alone** (p. 390 and p. 392), **both with
  OCR-flagged elements. These must be collated with the two non-agreeing publisher addresses already
  reported from chunk 001 — a human should check whether this range's two forms are the same pair or a
  third and fourth.**

#### H. Facts in this range that could not be placed in an archive row

**Counted as: distinct, page-referenced facts that bear on an archive row but could not be entered under
one — because the subject is unnamed in the range, because the name is bare, or because the row is
actively excluded. Total: 24.**

1–6. **The six facts of the unnamed Kalpi saint** (pp. 304–305, folios 300–301), which attach to nobody
until chunk 015 is read: pir **حضرت سید امیر ابو العلی**; affiliation **نسبت ابو العلائیہ /
طریقہ عالیہ نقش بندیہ ابو العلائیہ**; death **26 شعبان 1071ھ**; mazar at **کالپی**; son and sajjada
nashin **حضرت سید احمد**; second khalifa **شیخ محمد افضل**.

7. **سلطان باہو as a bare name in an anecdote** (p. 349, folio 345) — cf. `garh-maharaja-shorkot`.
8. **حضرت خواجہ محمد سلیمان as a bare name in a khalifa list** (p. 348, folio 344) — cf.
   `darbar-hazrat-khawaja-shah-muhammad-sulaiman-taunsvi-r-a`.
9. **حافظ محمد جمال ملتانی as a bare name in the same list** (p. 348, folio 344) — cf.
   `shrine-of-hafiz-muhammad-jamal-multani`. **The likeliest of the bare-name cases to be genuine.**
10. **The Pakpattan visit with no shrine attached** (p. 348, folio 344) — cf.
    `shrine-of-fariduddin-ganjshakar`.
11. **قاضی محمد عاقل's madrasa at کوٹ مٹھن** (p. 363, folio 359) — cf. `mithankot-kot-mithan`.
12. **The bai'at at اچ** (p. 364, folio 360) — five Uch rows, none of their figures, shrines or dates
    named. **Never pick.**
13. **حضرت شاہ ابو المعالی of "اینتھہ [OCR?]"** (pp. 326–328, folios 322–324) — `darbar-abul-muali-qadri`
    actively excluded on location and chronology.
14. **"میران" / "میران جی" of کہرام** (p. 329, folio 325) — `shrine-of-mauj-darya-bukhari` excluded on

15. **حضرت شاہ نیاز احمد's name inside `wasif-ali-wasif`'s silsila field** (pp. 356, 358, 360;

    `khawaja-feroz-ud-din-gharib-nawaz`.

19. **مہار / مہارو and خواجہ نور محمد مہاروی's mazar** (p. 348, folio 344) — a mazar statement with no

20–23. **The four advertisement bare names matching rows** (p. 392): حضرت سلطان باہو; حضرت بلھے شاہ;
حضرت بابا فرید الدین مسعود گنج شکر; خواجہ شاہ حسین.

24. **The one advertisement line that *was* mapped** — "خواجہ غلام فرید کوٹ مٹھن شریف" (p. 392) —

**Not counted here, because the merge in section A removes the anonymity even though no archive row
results**: the material of the Kahram entry (pp. 325–331, folios 321–327), the Bareilly entry
